# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- [#212](../../issues/212) - `npx omega test --lane=stripe-live`: an opt-in lane running the payment pipeline against REAL Stripe test-mode events, forwarded by `stripe listen`. Opens only for an `sk_test_` secret resolved through the one env reader, else prints one skip line. Idempotent, tagged product/price fixtures.
- [#212](../../issues/212) - A test dispute provider, so a chargeback is provable end to end: an alert matches by amount and card against the emulator's records, then issues the cancel as the `customer.subscription.deleted` event Stripe's own cancel produces. Refuses in production.
- [#212](../../issues/212) - `POST /payments/intent` takes `simulate: 'abandon'` - the session is created and no webhook is fired, which is what an abandoned checkout IS. Test-provider only, never persisted.

### Changed
- [#1011](../../issues/1011) - Each email provider's facts live in one frozen row in config (`email-providers.js`): its role, env key, native fields and operations. Callers reach a provider through its role (`campaigns`, `newsletter`), the per-field `skip` lists are gone, and an unknown role word in `providers` fails by name.
- [#1010](../../issues/1010) - Each analytics provider's facts live in one frozen row in config (`analytics-providers.js`), baked for browsers as `analytics.capabilities`. The provider is named `google` everywhere: the `ga4` adapter file, catalog key and provider strings are renamed.
- [#1013](../../issues/1013) - Each target type's rules live in one frozen row in config (`target-types.js`): framework, walk order, deploy mode, publish flag, verify legs and defaults. The schema's `TARGETS`, the manager's deploy, publish, verify and ship lists and devkit's twin derive from the rows; the old copies are gone.
- [#851](../../issues/851) - The snap is named `brand.id`: the build pins it, the Snap Store link derives from it, and the manager's publishing service registers the name or prints the manual step. A Linux package or release without the snap login refuses by name. The download page lists the enabled formats.
- [#905](../../issues/905) - One call, `translateCached` in `@omega.js/devkit/translate`, does the cache lookup, the cache-only switch, the live call and the save for the website and both extension passes. A CI build reads saved translations only. The extension's store description saves as `translations/<lang>/description.json`.
- [#1124](../../issues/1124) - The root packages lane runs the package suites in a pool with a serial tail, and the test runner runs files side by side under process isolation. CI setup lives in `hooks.ciSetup`, and a CI run narrows the root `npm test` to the packages lane.
- [#1124](../../issues/1124) - CI runs through the kit's caller workflow. Its `release` job makes each GitHub release and tag once a release commit reaches `main`, and the ship's cut step only waits for it.
- [#1148](../../issues/1148) - A brand on a default GitHub Pages host has one API address, its project's Cloud Functions `omega_api` url, answered by config's `apiAddress` on every surface and in the manager's webhooks. It derives no support email or contact block, and the streams and advertising steps skip hosts it does not own.
- [#1147](../../issues/1147) - Before each deploy the snapshot chain is re-rooted on GitHub's side through the git data API: the deploy ref's tip becomes a parentless copy of its own tree and the new snapshot chains on it, so the ref holds one root, the latest snapshot and any admin publish since.
- [#830](../../issues/830) - Jekyll-era template filters take `omega_*` names (`omega_slugify`, `omega_date_xmlschema`, `omega_where` and the rest). An old name fails the build naming its replacement, the `compat-filters` rule of `omega migrate` rewrites them, and `omega_jsonify` is compact by default (pass an indent for pretty output).
- [#1084](../../issues/1084) - The `omega:init` skill ends on one commit holding the scaffold, made once the local site answers and before any port or feature work, so every brand has a clean starting point.
- [#609](../../issues/609) - The playground carries four `_alternatives` fixtures so /alternatives and its comparison pages are checkable, and drops both its hand-written `download` map (derived) and its `translation.exclude` list (framework-owned).

### Fixed
- [#947](../../issues/947) - A desktop app that quits inside its `second-instance` handler no longer strands the next launch: the quit waits for the forwarded launch to exit (5 second limit), and a boot that stalls for 60 seconds logs its last step and exits.
- [#1161](../../issues/1161) - One newsletter run creates one Beehiiv post: `sendCampaign` owns the publish, and the generator returns `contentTags` and a `report` instead of posting itself.
- [#1162](../../issues/1162) - The monthly re-engagement email carries its text and goes only to the campaigns provider, which holds the engagement segments.
- [#1163](../../issues/1163) - A campaign whose segment key resolves to nothing, or whose audience resolves to nobody, fails with a coded 400 naming the key, and nothing is sent. A multi-line segment query is read whole.
- [#1149](../../issues/1149) - The manager's edge email-ask suite reads an answered prompt by either tick glyph the prompt library draws, so its seven cases pass under a plain `TERM=linux` terminal as well as under `xterm-256color`.
- [#1169](../../issues/1169) - A DNS server failure (`ESERVFAIL`) no longer blocks an email address: the `dns` check is skipped for it, like any other network error. A missing domain or missing mail records still block.
- [#1155](../../issues/1155) - The port allocator's free check also binds UDP on both wildcard families, so a port held by a UDP socket reads taken, the way firebase-tools on macOS reads it. The emulator port-retry suite's windows moved to 21100 through 21449, below the short-lived socket range.
- [#769](../../issues/769) - Windows hosts get answers, not crashes: one cross-platform PATH probe (`where`/`which`) behind every mkcert, nodemon and Stripe-CLI check, mkcert and openjdk install hints that branch per platform instead of always saying `brew`, every shelled-out child through the host's own shell, and no `sleep` or `lsof` assumed.
- [#212](../../issues/212) - The trial-lapse sweep re-reads and writes in ONE transaction, and counts a same-second webhook write as newer. A provider's own trial-end event landing in the sweep's window, or inside its read's own second, was silently written over.
- [#212](../../issues/212) - The four test processors write `metadata.created`/`completed` like the webhook route, not `received`/`processed`. Their synthetic events handed the pipeline's staleness clock nothing and rode a now-fallback as if freshly arrived.

## [0.57.0] - 2026-11-04

### Added
- [#1145](../../issues/1145) [`133de8da`](../../commit/133de8da) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An unset `brand.url` on a GitHub Pages web brand resolves to its Pages address in the config loader, a sibling web target derives its own, and every domain-managing service (edge, domain, search, cloud, account, campaigns, captcha, verify, purge) skips a default Pages host before any credential ask.
- [#822](../../issues/822) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every build warns, never fails, on a bare `require` of a package the target did not declare, naming both fixes: declare it, or load the framework's copy through its door (`omega.require`, `require('@omega.js/desktop').require`, the extension twin). Backend aligns its two firebase peers on every verb.
- [#955](../../issues/955) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand's own top-level user keys (`users/{uid}.notes`) ride into `User` on every surface: the account root passes unknown keys through, a stored key named like a `User` property throws naming it, and the framework rules refuse a client write of one. The playground counts notes on the user document.
- [#1120](../../issues/1120) [`23a2720c`](../../commit/23a2720c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand ships its own Cloudflare Worker: `script` resolves in the brand's `workers/` folder, then the company tree's, then the framework's, and a worker entry's `secrets: ['KEY']` sends brand `.env` values as `secret_text` bindings. A missing script or secret fails the walk before the workers step sends any request.
- [#1116](../../issues/1116) [`5456f642`](../../commit/5456f642) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/web/test` gains the Node half of the framework-deps rule: `require(name)` answers from web's own module context, the twin of backend's `omega.require`, and `loadModule(modulePath, { omega, document, window })` bundles one page module through the asset build's alias plugin. A brand's test never names a framework dependency.
- [#709](../../issues/709) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Any element carrying `data-omega-copy` is a copy control, with no page JS: one delegated handler resolves the value - an explicit `data-omega-copy-value`/property, a selector, else the sibling input - copies it and reports it. Every hand-wired copy on the account page is gone ([#727](../../issues/727)).

### Changed
- [#1127](../../issues/1127) [`31943425`](../../commit/31943425) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `{{ path }}` tokens render through devkit's one `tokens` module on desktop, extension and every scaffold; a token nothing resolves fails the desktop html task naming the view and the token, as the extension's already did. Desktop's custom-brackets option is gone.
- [#1130](../../issues/1130) [`31943425`](../../commit/31943425) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Six twins collapse to one home: the devlog's and backend's slugifiers are the client module (so "Don't" slugs as `dont`), the test redirect route checks `isSitePath`, `.env` is spelled once as devkit's `ENV_FILE`, `output()` is a `BaseCommand` method, `STOP_GRACE_MS` lives in `emulator-orphans.js` alone.
- [#1134](../../issues/1134) [`31943425`](../../commit/31943425) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend port-retry suite boots the real firebase-tools child on a throwaway project off the classic ports: a held port forces firebase's own port-taken exit, so the retry and the double-failure report are proved on the real path and the scripted child is gone.
- [#852](../../issues/852) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The pricing promo close is a listener in the pricing module; the Product JSON-LD block gates on `schema.product.enabled` and prices from the lowest plan shown; the showcase index paginates every gallery (`components.html` is gone); the brandmark URL and the team link icon each have one include.
- [#853](../../issues/853) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Backend CLI verbs log through devkit's Logger; one port-release poll lives in `emulator-orphans.js`; request-side log redaction runs the credential family test at any depth; `slugify`, `isSitePath` and the brand host have one home in `@omega.js/client/modules`; the signup limit reads from config.
- [#854](../../issues/854) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The four CI workflow templates share one header; the extension's `publish.js` reads the store CLI's stdout, its tokens and the leftover check each have one home, and nine build suites share one helper; desktop's CDP client clears its timers and watches the session through devkit's `whileLive`.
- [#855](../../issues/855) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every production JSON write goes through devkit's `writeJson` (two-space indent, one trailing newline); `monorepo-root` and `cdp-live` are devkit modules; `deploy-snapshot` reports its `failureDetail`; mcp-router runs from `dist/`, devkit vendored at prepare; the plugin README documents the `shape` hook.
- [#969](../../issues/969) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega test` at the brand root forwards `--offline`, `--filter` and `--layer`; the dev legs pass the root's flags to each target; the manager's verb `ALIASES` derive from devkit's registry, each entry carrying a `passThrough` field; brand.md states `omega dev --target=backend` covers `serve`.
- [#1126](../../issues/1126) [`ee0ea3c2`](../../commit/ee0ea3c2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `engines.node` is `>=24` on web, desktop, extension, manager, mcp-router and template-kit; the Functions-side packages stay `>=22` and `omega.functionsRuntime` stays `"22"`; the extension's `omega.nodeRuntime` is 24. A brand on Node 22: `docs/shared/breaking-changes.md` has the row.
- [#831](../../issues/831) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The auth flow's return destination is `returnUrl` on every hop (`/signin`, `/signup`, `/reset`, `/token`, the desktop and extension auth URLs, the backend CLI's printed URL), the name the connections flow already used; `authReturnUrl` is gone, with no compat read.
- [#824](../../issues/824) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `brand.contact.person` is gone: the schema, `omega onboard`, the campaigns walk and the backend's welcome, discount-nudge and checkup sends no longer need a person. The sends sign off as the brand's team, and `omega migrate` deletes the block from an old config.
- [#832](../../issues/832) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - TikTok commerce events carry `content_category` on every item, from the product type (`subscription` or `one-time`) the web already sends and the backend's payment and trial-lapse events now send too.
- [#820](../../issues/820) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop and extension guides state the view-declaration fact beside web's and backend's: a page or route is a file, a desktop view opens through `omega.windows.create()`, an extension view through the manifest or a runtime call. Nothing auto-registers a view.
- [#967](../../issues/967) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend guide names `metadata.created.timestampUNIX`, a whole-seconds number, as the newest-first order key on every listing, so two records created in the same second have no defined order.
- [#828](../../issues/828) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web charts helper draws every chart through one entry, `chart(id, kind, data, options)`; `barChart`, `stackedBarChart`, `doughnutChart` and `lineChart` are gone. A shape the four kinds lack is an `options.definition` function on the page.
- [#833](../../issues/833) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page's usage block is one card per counted feature: the month figure as the headline, a bar drawn from theme tokens, the day figure, and when each counter resets in the viewer's local time.
- [#821](../../issues/821) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `llms.txt` is now an llmstxt.org brand summary: the brand name, description, tagline and contact email, then Pages, Posts, Social and Downloads (desktop files and store listings) sections. Empty sections are dropped, and a site-wide noindex removes every link.

### Removed
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`748ec89b`](../../commit/748ec89b) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The suite converter (`scripts/convert-suites.js`, its source and assert layers, its fixtures) is gone: every `node:test` suite it was written for is converted, and a brand's remaining suite converts by hand with the register's map.

### Fixed
- [#1151](../../issues/1151) [`133de8da`](../../commit/133de8da) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend port-retry suite boots each throwaway project on the first 70-port window nothing on the machine holds, instead of a blind pick the boot's preflight then refuses, and names the range when every window is taken.
- [#1150](../../issues/1150) [`133de8da`](../../commit/133de8da) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manager's emulator-teardown test gives a slow init five seconds to reap the signalled group, so a container that collects orphans on a tick no longer reads a sound teardown as a leader-only signal.
- [#1144](../../issues/1144) [`133de8da`](../../commit/133de8da) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The deploy snapshot is parented on the deploy ref's remote tip and pushed without force, so a push carries only what changed since the last deploy; the first snapshot of a repo stays parentless.
- [#1146](../../issues/1146) [`133de8da`](../../commit/133de8da) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand deploy summary gives a target that stepped aside its own `⊘ <name>: <detail>` row, and prints "N target(s) not attempted after the failure" only when a failing backend stopped the run.
- [#1128](../../issues/1128) [`31943425`](../../commit/31943425) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Claude plugin's MCP launcher checks for the router's `dist/router.js` before requiring it; a never-prepared checkout gets one stderr line naming `npm run prepare --prefix <pkg root>` and exit 1, instead of a module-not-found stack and no MCP server.
- [#1099](../../issues/1099) [`399d3d10`](../../commit/399d3d10) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend emulator boot's one bump-and-retry now fires on firebase's own "Port N is not open" exit as well as node's `EADDRINUSE`, still only for a port this run allocated. Two stacks booting in the same second no longer stop the boot.
- [#1100](../../issues/1100) [`399d3d10`](../../commit/399d3d10) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - In the flows lane a failed boot case (ports held, emulator, website) skips every later case with the reason, instead of running each into its 60 s wait; the matrix rows keep reporting independently.
- [#960](../../issues/960) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A failed desktop sign-out no longer says "Successfully signed out": main's sign-out rethrows the Firebase error, the renderer's `omega.signOut()` rejects, and the client's sign-out button shows its failure notice.
- [#952](../../issues/952) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend's setup check reads the staged manifest at `dist/package.json`, where staging writes it, and compares the same derived dependency map (`file:` specs rewritten one level up), so a converged target no longer rewrites and restages on every setup.
- [#993](../../issues/993) [`cf7d3ec8`](../../commit/cf7d3ec8) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The dispute-alert retry test asserts the route's promise and the retry's rewrite, never a status the Firestore trigger overwrites a moment later, so the backend suite no longer fails on that race.
- [#954](../../issues/954) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page under `src/pages/` with no `permalink` now serves at its file path (`notes.md` serves at `/notes`, written as `notes.html`) and overrides a default page at that URL, instead of every such page writing to one conflicting output.
- [#964](../../issues/964) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Running `omega translate` again over the same `dist/` now replaces each page's hreflang and `og:locale:alternate` tags instead of adding to them, and leaves the files byte-identical. Copies and originals list only the languages that pass produced.
- [#827](../../issues/827) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every language the translation engine supports now gets a language-switcher flag. Farsi and Greek show their country flags, and languages whose country has no flag in the set show a neutral globe instead of no image.
- [#974](../../issues/974) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A fresh web target's `omega test` no longer fails on dead account anchors: the legal pages now link `/dashboard/account#data-request`, `#delete` and `#refund`, the account page's own section routes.
- [#833](../../issues/833) [`490bcd8a`](../../commit/490bcd8a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend's daily cron now runs at midnight UTC, not midnight Pacific, so usage counters reset when the account page says they do.
- [#1118](../../issues/1118) [`5456f642`](../../commit/5456f642) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega dev` runs the target checks before it boots, offline (as `omega test --offline` does), so a fresh brand's first `npm start` installs the `firebase-functions` and `firebase-admin` peers its `file:` link left out. The journey lane now waits for the seeded line and fails on a seed warning.
- [#1119](../../issues/1119) [`5456f642`](../../commit/5456f642) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend's overlay watch test touches an input and waits for the watcher's own re-stage line before it writes the edit it asserts on, closing the one-time miss where the edit landed before the directory's event stream armed.
- [#1101](../../issues/1101) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every test that reads a verb log runs its verb inside devkit's `withoutCI`, which clears the CI flags for the call. On a runner the CI tee is a no-op, so each such case read an empty log and failed.
- [#1102](../../issues/1102) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Certificate fixtures are minted by devkit's `mintCert`, through `openssl ca -selfsign` with an explicit validity window. The old fixtures used openssl 3.4-only flags, so LibreSSL on a Mac and the runner's openssl both refused them.
- [#1103](../../issues/1103) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The config guide and the web README no longer link into the gitignored `_attic` folder: two links are gone and two point at the CHANGELOG's Phase 2 tasks. The root docs-links test, which checks every link target exists, passes on a clean checkout.
- [#1104](../../issues/1104) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `checks.yml` provisions what the package suites need: Java 21 and firebase-tools for the backend emulators, `xvfb-run` for the desktop window, node 24 with the npm cache, a 60-minute timeout, and a superseded run is cancelled.
- [#1105](../../issues/1105) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop's test runner spawns Electron with `--no-sandbox` on a Linux CI runner, through one `electronArgs` helper both spawn sites use. Electron's SUID sandbox refuses to start on ubuntu-latest, so the main layer never booted there.
- [#1106](../../issues/1106) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The three web watch suites wait for the watcher to list their file before the first edit, through `waitForWatched`. chokidar fires ready before every root is walked when a root is missing, so an early edit was lost on Linux.
- [#1107](../../issues/1107) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - devkit's port hold binds the two loopback addresses only and asks the allocator's own `isPortFree` first. On Linux the wildcard bind collided with the hold's own loopback bind, so a held port read as free.
- [#1108](../../issues/1108) [`800af2d7`](../../commit/800af2d7) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manager's fake-gh helper symlinks the real git into its temp bin, so hiding `gh` no longer hides `git`. On a runner both share one bin folder, and the PATH filter dropped git with gh.
- [#1117](../../issues/1117) [`2016ffa6`](../../commit/2016ffa6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend self-test seeds personas four at a time instead of all 91 at once, since the functions emulator starts one uncapped worker per in-flight auth trigger and a 7 GB CI runner ran out of memory. The seed also stops on the first persona whose `auth:on-create` never completes.
- [#1123](../../issues/1123) [`748ec89b`](../../commit/748ec89b) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension's background lane fails a suite by name (`service worker for <id> is not active`) the moment its worker dies mid-suite, instead of waiting out the 60 s case timeout. A worker killed mid-command reports only `Inspector.targetCrashed`, so the gone-worker case hung on the ubuntu runner.

## [0.56.0] - 2026-10-13

### Added
- [#1032](../../issues/1032) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Each enabled target is checked for the machine tools it needs (Java and the Firebase CLI for a Firebase backend), from one devkit list. The wizard, `omega dev` and `omega manage` name a missing tool with its install command, `omega status` lists it; only the emulator and the deploy refuse.
- [#1047](../../issues/1047) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand-root `omega update` that moves the `@omega.js/manager` pin ends on the by-hand steps it owes: every entry of the breaking-changes register dated on or after the old version's publish day, newest first, then the path of the installed register.
- [#1039](../../issues/1039) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npm run manage` asks for the registrar, the email provider, the first forwarding rule and whether the Firebase project is shared, and writes each answer to `config/omega.json5`. Disable at an email ask writes `false`, which means none. A headless run prints one line naming the key.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/devkit` holds one test runner for `defineCases` suites, built on `node:test` in one process (`@omega.js/devkit/test/runner`, with `/test/cli` as its command line), with one layer registry and one reporter. The nine library packages and the root scripts run on it; the frameworks follow in their own groups.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The runner takes `--isolation=none|process`: under `process` each suite file runs in its own child, with `--filter`, the log tee and the reporters unchanged. A case registers `ctx.after(fn)`, run in reverse once the case settles, and a suite carries `setup(ctx)` beside `cleanup`, once before the first case.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `node scripts/convert-suites.js <path>... [--check] [--harness=<module>]` rewrites a plain `node:test` suite in place into a `defineCases` group: hooks to `setup`/`cleanup`/`ctx.after`, every `assert` call to its `ctx.expect` matcher, a module-scope helper's to a file-level `expect`, `--harness` requiring both from one module; anything else is refused by name.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `expect` moves to `@omega.js/devkit/test/expect` (replacing `/test/assert`) and grows: a message argument, `.rejects`, `expect.fail`, the `OrEqual` comparisons, `toBeTypeOf`, `toBeSuccess`, `toBeError`, `toHaveProperty` with a path and a value, and a `toThrow` that is sync on a sync function. `toBe` uses `Object.is`; `toEqual` is strict deep equality.
- [#1061](../../issues/1061) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A user links several accounts of one provider: the stored shape is `connections.<provider>.<identityId>`, `connections.<provider>.max` (default 1) caps them, refresh, status and disconnect take an `id`, the account page draws one card per account, and the users migration moves old records (breaking-changes register).
- [#1070](../../issues/1070) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `edge.providers.cloudflare.webAnalytics.enabled` (default `true`): the edge walk's `web-analytics` operation makes the zone's Cloudflare Web Analytics site match it, creating the site with automatic install when missing. A token without "Account Settings Write" warns and the walk goes on.

### Changed
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The root e2e lanes join the one devkit runner: each is a `defineCases` suite under `test/e2e/`, one case per step. A brand's `test/e2e/` holds suite files the brand-root `omega test` runs in-process after the targets (breaking-changes register). No lane writes a `steps.log`: `grep '✗' .temp/logs/<lane>.log` names the failing case.
- [#931](../../issues/931) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `OMEGA_TEST_MODE`, `OMEGA_TEST_RUNNER` and `OMEGA_CASE_RUNNER` are gone from code, tests and docs: every lane sets `OMEGA_ENVIRONMENT=testing` and every reader calls `isTesting()`. A repo-wide ratchet fails any file that names one.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every suite of the nine library packages and of the root `scripts/` is a `defineCases` group run by the one devkit runner: each `test` script is `node ../devkit/src/test/cli.js` (process isolation for `client`, `config`, `devkit` and `manager`), devkit's `scripts/run-tests.js` is gone, and so is `OMEGA_CASE_RUNNER`.
- [#931](../../issues/931) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `OMEGA_ENVIRONMENT=testing` is the one name that says a test run is on, read through `isTesting()`: config no longer turns `OMEGA_TEST_MODE` into `testing`, the backend test and emulator lanes set the one name on their children, and monitoring kills reporting on `isTesting()` instead of `OMEGA_TEST_RUNNER` (breaking-changes register).
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Backend joins the one devkit runner: every suite runs in one process on the `emulator` layer and asserts with `expect` (`ctx.assert` is gone), `test/_init.js` is devkit's factory and a broken one fails the run, and `logs/test.log` is the one tee.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Web joins the one devkit runner: every web suite is a `defineCases` file asserting with `ctx.expect`, the new `@omega.js/web/test` export serves brands, the smoke checks are a `smoke` suite, files run in their own processes, `test/watch/` serial, and `omega test` takes `--filter`, `--layer`, `--lane`, `--extended` and `--reporter`.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Extension joins the one devkit runner: `omega test` runs every suite in one process, its Chrome drivers running background, view and built-view suites through devkit's own engine. A boot case is `{ name, run(ctx) }` with `ctx.extension` and `ctx.page`; a built-view suite declares `layer: 'boot'`. `--reporter=json` prints one document.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop joins the one devkit runner: `omega test` runs every suite in one process, its Electron drivers running main, renderer and boot suites through devkit's own engine. A boot case is `{ name, run(ctx) }` with `ctx.omega`; a built-view suite declares `layer: 'boot'` and gets `ctx.page`. `--reporter=json` prints one document.
- [#931](../../issues/931) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend test host boots the instance with `initialize({ runnerHost: true })`, which skips the Firebase init, the function wiring, the server and Sentry, in place of reading `OMEGA_TEST_RUNNER` (breaking-changes register).
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Backend `omega test --reporter=json` prints one JSON document on stdout; every other line of the verb goes to stderr. Boot prelude notes (linked docs sync, origin heal) print on stderr on every framework. A failed pre-flight ends the run with one error line.
- [#1058](../../issues/1058) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Root `npm test` runs every lane whatever each one's result and ends on a summary: one row per lane with its mark and time, then how many failed. The run exits with the first failed lane's code. Every lane with more than one command does the same.
- [#1054](../../issues/1054) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One line tells an owner how to make a repo addressable: `repoMissing(role)` in `@omega.js/config`, quoted by all nine refusals (backend, desktop, devkit, extension, web) after each one's own lead. It names the repo by role: source, releases or website.
- [#1041](../../issues/1041) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The repo block's owner key is `repo.owner` (`repo: { provider: 'github', owner }`), and onboard's flag is `--owner`. The strict schema refuses `repo.org`, and `npx omega migrate --execute` moves it; the seo content item's override is `item.owner`.
- [#1046](../../issues/1046) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega update` holds no release of the `@omega.js/*` family for its first seven days: one allowlist (`QUARANTINE_EXEMPT` in devkit's `update.js`) names the packages the quarantine skips, so a brand takes a family release the day it ships while every other package keeps the hold.
- [#1049](../../issues/1049) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The deploy snapshot force-adds the staged framework tarballs, the lockfile and the resolved company file past any ignore rule, the way it already force-adds the minted logo set. The refusal that asked for a `.gitignore` edit is gone, and so is the root `!/brands/*/omega_modules/*.tgz` line.
- [#1037](../../issues/1037) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A signed-out `gh` in a headless manage walk skips the repo and seo services like a missing key, listed under "Missing secrets" as `GH_TOKEN`, instead of failing the walk. In a terminal it is still an error. The journey lane no longer allows a repo-service error.
- [#1044](../../issues/1044) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One injected process runner, `execFn(file, args, options)`, for the manager's `gh` and `claude` seams: `runOnboard` and the install verb take `options.execFn` (replacing `ghExec` and `claudeExec`), the workspace op's context key is `execFn`, and the manager's test tree keeps one `gh` fake.
- [#1038](../../issues/1038) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The journey lane's scrubbed legs point gh, firebase-tools and gcloud at empty config homes (`CLI_CONFIG_HOMES`), so a signed-in developer machine lends none of its stored logins to the run.
- [#1040](../../issues/1040) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The breaking-changes register names the three retired legacy flows a migrating brand may look for: the `--quick` task runner (nothing replaced it), the Stripe test-flow simulator (the payment journey tests and the `stripe-live` lane), and the `products`, `pricing-md` and `cdn` disperse paths.
- [#1033](../../issues/1033) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `docs/shared/publishing.md` says who creates a release's `v<version>` tag: the ship's GitHub release step, on the remote, one per release commit.
- [#1043](../../issues/1043) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `baseMissingKeys(env, { target, verb })` is the one answer to "which env keys does every brand owe"; `requiredEnvKeys` is gone.
- [#1035](../../issues/1035) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A default desktop format owes its keys: the Linux snap and `self-hosted` Windows signing ship unless turned off, so manage asks for `SNAPCRAFT_STORE_CREDENTIALS`, `WIN_EV_TOKEN_PATH` and `WIN_CSC_KEY_PASSWORD`, and publish and deploy refuse until they are set or the format is off.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `defineCases` file run directly, with `node --test <file>` or `node <file>`, runs its own cases instead of failing on load: a build- or node-layer suite runs in place, and a suite on a layer that needs the app or the emulator fails with one case naming `npx omega test`.
- [#984](../../issues/984) [`7953b730`](../../commit/7953b730) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `test/_init.js` hook that fails to load, or whose factory or `setup` throws, fails the run naming the file on web, desktop and extension, where it used to print the error and run the suites anyway.
- [#1069](../../issues/1069) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The signup form's terms checkbox also states the age: "I agree to <brand>'s Terms of Service and Privacy Policy, and I am at least 13 years old." One checkbox, no new config key; the stored consent record carries the same sentence.
- [#1065](../../issues/1065) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The README's Get started steps match where the reader is: step 1 links the brand template's "Use this template" page, step 2 says to clone the new repo, step 3 is `npm start`. The template's own README now teaches clone and `npm start` under a tip card.
- [#1062](../../issues/1062) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The client boots only the Firebase app and auth. Firestore and messaging load on first use through `omega.loadFirebaseFirestore()` and `omega.loadFirebaseMessaging()`, replacing the `firebaseFirestore` and `firebaseMessaging` getters (breaking-changes register). The Sentry SDK loads once the page is idle; an early-error buffer replays what it missed.
- [#1062](../../issues/1062) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every page writes a `modulepreload` link for each chunk in its script entries' static import chains, read from esbuild's output graph (`assetManifest.js.imports`). A chunk reached only by a dynamic import is never listed, so Firestore and Sentry stay off the first load.
- [#1062](../../issues/1062) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `/build.js` joins the default "Assets: Cache for 1 Year" edge cache rule, and the service worker's `importScripts` carries the build stamp (`__OMEGA_BUILD_STAMP__`, the head's `?cb=` value), so a browser never holds a year-old config past a build.
- [#1064](../../issues/1064) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every browser bundle compiles to the `es2020` floor by default (`BROWSER_TARGET` in devkit) and ships its source map beside the output in every mode, so Sentry reads public maps with no upload. A devkit guard test names any unguarded use of a newer browser API.
- [#1067](../../issues/1067) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - With `protocol.singleInstance: false` a desktop launch appends to `runtime.log` and tags every line `[pid <n>]`, since launches overlap; with the lock on, only the launch that wins the lock empties the file of earlier launches' lines, so a duplicate launch never erases the running copy's log.

### Fixed
- [#957](../../issues/957) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension build stops on an illegal match pattern in `src/manifest.json` (content scripts, host permissions, web-accessible resources), naming each one, instead of writing a manifest the browser refuses to load.
- [#1052](../../issues/1052) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Manage no longer deletes a brand's mail records before it asks: the edge service's setup asks for the mailbox provider before any operation, and with no provider picked (unset, Skip or Disable) `dns-records` leaves every existing MX record alone. Only a pick marks another provider's MX records as obsolete.
- [#1056](../../issues/1056) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An seo content item's unknown key is refused by the one strict check, in `config/omega.json5` and in `config/seo.json5`: the schema declares an item's keys, the check walks a declared list, and the line names the item (`seo.github.content[0].org`). `npx omega migrate --execute` moves an item's `org` to `owner` in both files.
- [#1050](../../issues/1050) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The first converge of an unmarked `test/README.md` keeps a brand's own code block and table whole, blank lines included, and keeps a heading whose section is all the brand's. It used to drop any line that matched a framework line: fences, brackets, separator rows and headings.
- [#1051](../../issues/1051) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A scrubbed test run cannot reach the Google default credentials file under the home folder: the scrub points `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` at a file that is never created, so Google's auth library fails instead of falling back. The scrub needs a throwaway home root, and every caller passes one.
- [#1055](../../issues/1055) [`d1f0a1dd`](../../commit/d1f0a1dd) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows cloud signing keys (`AZURE_*`, `SSLCOM_*`, `DIGICERT_*`) are owed only when a desktop target ships the installer, signs with the `cloud` strategy and names that provider. A provider written with the installer off no longer owes them.
- [#1059](../../issues/1059) [`ab43bfaf`](../../commit/ab43bfaf) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The config loader reads the brand's `config/seo.json5` as the `seo` section, with the same merge and the same two checks as `omega.json5`. An seo item names its token with `author.tokenEnv` (an `.env` variable; unset fails the item), and every path line spells a list position `[0]`.
- [#1045](../../issues/1045) [`c252c4d2`](../../commit/c252c4d2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The in-repo brands deploy again: the monorepo's `*.tgz` ignore rule hid their staged framework tarballs from the snapshot, and the runner's install failed. The snapshot now carries them whatever the ignore rules say (see #1049).
- [#1048](../../issues/1048) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The first converge of an unmarked `test/README.md` drops every line an earlier framework text carried, not only today's, so a brand that never wrote a note of its own ends with an empty Custom section instead of 20 lines of old framework prose.
- [#1034](../../issues/1034) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The website's CI image cache hits: the workflow caches `${{ github.workspace }}/.omega/cache/imagemin`, the folder the build writes, where the composed copy used to cache an empty `targets/web/` path and re-optimized every image on every run.
- [#1026](../../issues/1026) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A working `GH_TOKEN` is accepted: the one GitHub CLI check proves the login with `gh api user`, never `gh auth status`, which refuses a token it did not store. The repo and seo services, the devlog and the secrets publish all share it.
- [#1042](../../issues/1042) [`9fcaccfc`](../../commit/9fcaccfc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npm start` spawns no `claude`: the Claude plugin's install hint and update belong to the full manage walk, and one walk reads the machine once instead of two or three times.
- [#1063](../../issues/1063) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two accessibility failures on every classy page: `aria-busy` moves from the `<html>` element, which allows no ARIA attribute, to `<body>`; and the footer's column heads, copyright and "Powered by" lines use the muted ink color (5.2 to 1 in light, 6.9 to 1 in dark).
- [#1071](../../issues/1071) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend honors `connections.<provider>.enabled`: authorize, tokenize and refresh answer a 400 for a provider whose resolved value is `false`, where only the account page used to hide it. Status and disconnect still work. A brand using a packaged provider sets it `true` (see the breaking-changes register).
- [#1068](../../issues/1068) [`695d2451`](../../commit/695d2451) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A desktop brand with no cloud no longer logs `firebase init failed` as an error on every launch: with no `cloud.config.apiKey`, the main process logs one plain skip line and never loads Firebase, the rule the client already used.
- [#986](../../issues/986) [`e969d706`](../../commit/e969d706) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega.auth.reload()` waits for Firebase to restore the saved session before it reads the current user. A call right after a page load landed a signed-out state for a signed-in user.

## [0.55.0] - 2026-10-01

### Added
- [#818](../../issues/818) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A website target gets a scaffolded `test/README.md` and `test/_init.js` like the other three frameworks, and the web project lane runs the setup hook once before its suites. The playground website carries real build, built-output and browser tests.
- [#958](../../issues/958) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An extension's `src/manifest.json` speaks the `{{ }}` syntax a view does, rendered against the whole resolved config when the manifest compiles, so `matches: ['{{ brand.url }}/*']` follows `brand.url` instead of a hand-typed host that drifts. A token the config cannot resolve fails the build naming it.
- [#992](../../issues/992) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.desktop.protocol.singleInstance: false` turns the desktop single-instance lock off, so each launch of a CLI-shaped app runs as its own process. Unset reads as true; off, a second launch's argv and a Windows or Linux deep link never reach a running copy.
- [#1023](../../issues/1023) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand starts from the `Omega-JS-Stack/brand-template` repo: two files and `npm start` install OMEGA, run the wizard and boot the site. A file carrying the template marker is replaced by the generated one, and the brand id defaults from the repo name. The journey lane clones the real template.
- [#1031](../../issues/1031) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npx omega status` says what a folder is, what it lacks and the next command, from anywhere, writing nothing. The `omega:init` skill starts a brand from an empty folder, and `npx omega onboard` takes `--company` and `--admins`.
- [#1029](../../issues/1029) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `missingEnvKeys(config, env, { target, verb })` in `@omega.js/config` is the one answer to which `.env` keys a brand still owes, by target, config and verb. Every verb's env check reads it, so a website-only brand is never asked for a backend key.
- [#1028](../../issues/1028) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A consumer gets the omega Claude plugin from GitHub (`omega@omega`); an omega developer gets the checkout's copy (`omega@omega-local`) through `omega i local`. Onboarding offers the machine-wide install, every brand's settings name its copy, and the manager no longer vendors the plugin.

### Changed
- [#904](../../issues/904) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every sign-in lane (web, desktop, extension, the token wire lane) fetches its own seeded persona from `GET /omega/test/roster?machinery=true` (only a testing backend honours it), signs in with the test password, and asserts its own surface. No suite creates accounts or reads credentials.
- [#1015](../../issues/1015) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Registrar and mailbox providers are exactly the rows of one manager registry, `services/domain/lib/providers.js`. An unlisted `domain.email.providers` key, once accepted, now fails the load naming `npx omega migrate`; a `squarespace` registrar, once refused, now loads. Listed providers get the same DNS records (by-hand step: `docs/shared/breaking-changes.md`).
- [#857](../../issues/857) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The registry rule is a standing ruling in `docs/shared/rulings.md`: a family of like things keeps its per-member rules in one registry that every consumer reads, with a test pinning it to the member files. Each family still lacking one has its own issue.
- [#843](../../issues/843) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The scaffolded `test/README.md` has one source per framework (`src/defaults/test/README.md`), merged into every consumer copy by the scaffold walk on each verb under the Default/Custom markers, so a source fix reaches existing projects and a project's own notes under Custom survive.
- [#1009](../../issues/1009) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/backend` and `@omega.js/mcp-router` declare an `exports` map, so a deep path into either is refused. A backend case file takes `defineCases` from `@omega.js/backend/test`; the plugin launches the router through `@omega.js/mcp-router/cli`. The by-hand steps are in `docs/shared/breaking-changes.md`.
- [#1009](../../issues/1009) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The plugin guard refuses a brand backend route or schema that shadows the framework's in a registry install too: it reads the published `dist/` tree instead of `src/`, which the package never ships.
- [#1009](../../issues/1009) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/desktop/test` and `@omega.js/extension/test` hand out `{ defineCases, expect }`, and every desktop and extension case file in the scaffold and the playground brand wraps in `defineCases`, as backend case files do.
- [#842](../../issues/842) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The local port chain and every local URL live once, `@omega.js/client/modules/dev-ports.js`; the client class, desktop and extension call it. Every plain-http emulator URL answers `127.0.0.1` on every surface; the mkcert proxy stays on `localhost`.
- [#844](../../issues/844) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The scroll-reveal stagger (its 60ms default step and delay formula) has one home, `@omega.js/client/modules/reveal-stagger.js`. The motion engine imports it and the web build prints it into the head's inline first-paint starter, so a first-paint band and a scrolled-to band always stagger alike.
- [#966](../../issues/966) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every place devkit and the manager look for an installed package runs one node_modules walk, devkit's `resolvePackageDir` and `resolvePackageRealDir`, instead of ten hand-written copies, so the pack step, the bin lookup, the update fingerprint and the test service can no longer disagree about which copy a brand installed.
- [#846](../../issues/846) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The four framework guides name their in-repo playground target as the test consumer, in the same words; the legacy repo names are gone. `scripts/guide-mirror.test.js` keeps the guides' shared sections and their order in lockstep.
- [#1030](../../issues/1030) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npx omega onboard` scaffolds the website alone by default, accepts `--targets=none`, and a rerun adds a target along with the brand-level keys a fresh scaffold writes. `omega dev` on a brand with no targets exits clean with one line.
- [#889](../../issues/889) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Onboarding asks which GitHub owner holds the brand's repos, ordered as omega-manager did: create new, the repo's holder, a name match, then alphabetical; `--org` answers it headless. The origin's owner is the default when `gh` cannot answer.
- [#1024](../../issues/1024) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The README is written for a new user: the hero, a Use this template button, the three-step start, the features and the package table. What a contributor needs moved to `CONTRIBUTING.md`.

### Fixed
- [#1027](../../issues/1027) [`01461035`](../../commit/01461035) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The minted logo and favicon set reach the CI build: the snapshot lane force-adds the bridged sources, and a web build on CI fails naming the missing favicon set instead of deploying a site that 404s its logo, favicon and social image.
- [#956](../../issues/956) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An extension's background restores its own Firebase session when the service worker restarts and lands `auth.user` before it answers any message, so a content script is no longer refused after a worker wake. The account read gives up after 10 seconds, and a failed restore still releases the waiting messages.
- [#957](../../issues/957) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Firefox extension artifact drops every Chrome-only permission, `offscreen` now beside `sidePanel`, from one list in the package task. A brand declaring `offscreen` shipped it to Firefox, which has no offscreen API and rejects the unknown permission at install.
- [#959](../../issues/959) [`d7c437a2`](../../commit/d7c437a2) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An extension view suite that declares `view: '<name>'` runs in the project's own built view inside its packaged extension, the desktop `view:` shape; the harness pages stay for `context:`. A standalone view suite runs as one test on extension and desktop, and `--filter` no longer drops it.
- [#972](../../issues/972) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `seo.enabled`, `certificates.enabled` and `account.enabled` default to true in the schema, not in the manager's table; the Cloudflare engine data and `account.admins` moved there too. A test pins that no manager default shadows a schema default.
- [#1014](../../issues/1014) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows signing strategies and cloud providers are declared once in `@omega.js/config`; the schema, sign-windows, validate-certs, the build default, the runners check and the scaffolded workflow read it. The validator refuses an unknown cloud provider.
- [#841](../../issues/841) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The users migration derives its record, validation, nullable list and null-reset (number, boolean and timestamp fields only) from `@omega.js/account`'s schema, so new fields backfill without a hand copy. A null status is reported, never guessed.
- [#849](../../issues/849) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `prices.once` is the only one-time price key; `amount` migrates, a cadence on a one-time product is refused. One capability row per payment provider drives every backend gate and the checkout buttons; the rows are baked into the page.
- [#1012](../../issues/1012) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The connections card defaults can no longer drift from the providers `@omega.js/backend` ships: a config test fails when a packaged provider file has no default card, or a default card has no provider file, so enabling a packaged provider is always one line.
- [#848](../../issues/848) [`a926405d`](../../commit/a926405d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The self-containment gate reads one reference shape everywhere: CI's pack-smoke now runs the same whole-file scan as `npm run release:check`, so a raw private reference written `require( '@omega.js/config')`, with `from` ending its line, or as `import (  '@omega.js/analytics')` fails the build instead of shipping a tarball that breaks on install.
- [#1007](../../issues/1007) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A held e2e port answers nothing: the hold closes every connection it accepts, so a passed run exits at once instead of waiting on a browser tab still connected to a classic port like `localhost:5002`.
- [#1008](../../issues/1008) [`cb802e32`](../../commit/cb802e32) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `require('@omega.js/extension')` resolves again: the package root is the build-time entry `{ version, build }`, as it is on desktop, so the release check no longer marks the extension "resolve failed". The extension also exports `./test`, its public test API, as desktop does.

## [0.54.1] - 2026-09-30

### Fixed
- [#1004](../../issues/1004) [`d24aaf9e`](../../commit/d24aaf9e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The client's verts rotation test waits for two ticks under a 2 s ceiling instead of counting them after a fixed 90 ms sleep, so a loaded machine's late timer no longer fails it.
- [#1005](../../issues/1005) [`d24aaf9e`](../../commit/d24aaf9e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The flows lane's session-kill step brings the tab back into view every 5 s inside its 60 s wait, as a person would, and a failure names the client's last probe log line instead of a bare timeout.

## [0.54.0] - 2026-09-29

### Added
- [#995](../../issues/995) [`e01d67ee`](../../commit/e01d67ee) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A subagent spawned in an OMEGA project is handed the omega skills as `SKILL.md` paths to read, since it may have no Skill tool. At a brand root that covers every target plus the framework map, ending on the command that lets the edit gate through. Once per subagent.

### Changed
- [#996](../../issues/996) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand's `AGENTS.md` imports `node_modules/@omega.js/manager/AGENTS.md`, which imports the map, `docs/omega.md`, from the docs the manager ships; the monorepo root imports the same map. The next manage removes the old scope symlink, and a linked manager's docs sync from the monorepo before every omega command ([docs/shared/agent-docs.md](docs/shared/agent-docs.md)).
- [#997](../../issues/997) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every package's hand-written docs moved to the top-level `docs/<framework>/`, and only `@omega.js/manager` ships docs: the whole `docs/` tree. A path that named `@omega.js/<framework>/docs/` now names `@omega.js/manager/docs/<framework>/`.
- [#998](../../issues/998) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One gitignore pattern for the tree: lines the packages share live once in the root `.gitignore` (every package's `dist/` is one `packages/*/dist/` line), and a package file keeps only its own lines, each under a reason comment. Client, extension and web keep an empty `.gitignore`; the ignored set is unchanged.
- [#999](../../issues/999) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every project root, a brand or one framework used alone, gets the same `AGENTS.md`: it imports `node_modules/@omega.js/manager/AGENTS.md`, then holds your notes. An old-template file converges on the next verb, notes kept, and a brand target gets none. A standalone project adds `@omega.js/manager` as a dev dependency ([docs/shared/agent-docs.md](docs/shared/agent-docs.md#supported-shape)).
- [#1000](../../issues/1000) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every framework-written file mixing framework and consumer lines (project-root `AGENTS.md`, brand and company `.gitignore` and `.env`, target `.gitignore`, web `.gitattributes`) uses Default/Custom marker sections: the framework rewrites Default, Custom stays verbatim and starts empty. Older files converge on the next verb ([docs/shared/breaking-changes.md](docs/shared/breaking-changes.md)).
- [#991](../../issues/991) [`e01d67ee`](../../commit/e01d67ee) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The gate deadline env line is gone from `.claude/settings.json`: the commit gate no longer runs the suite itself, so nothing read it. The playground targets' own suites run by hand from the brand root, not at the commit.
- [#983](../../issues/983) [`6c7d9c96`](../../commit/6c7d9c96) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega dev` is one verb on all four frameworks, and every target's `start` script is `omega dev`, the one leg the brand-root boot runs. The backend's unit suites run inside `omega test` under the runner's connect trap; a custom-server backend runs its suites with no emulator.
- [#985](../../issues/985) [`b938afb1`](../../commit/b938afb1) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A verb runs inside a target its framework owns (`npm test`, `npm start`), a brand-wide verb refuses there, and framework source refuses every verb. Every brand-root fan-out runs each target's own `npm run <verb>`, custom targets included, and the target scripts derive from the one verb table.
- [#863](../../issues/863) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Verbs run from a root: `--target=` picks the target or package. A framework's suite runs from the monorepo root (`npx omega test --target=web framework:`); one devkit table declares every verb.
- [#863](../../issues/863) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand root runs every framework verb: `npx omega translate --target=web` reaches the web target's own CLI, arguments as typed. `omega i local` and `omega i live` run once at the brand root, and the fan-outs and the dispatcher read one verb table.
- [#885](../../issues/885) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One `omega migrate` at the brand root converts the brand config, then every target in the verb table's order (`--target=` narrows). A bare run reports and exits 1 while a step is due; `--execute` converts, then loads the brand config and exits 1 on any error.
- [#859](../../issues/859) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The config schema is strict: an undeclared key fails the load naming `npx omega migrate`, the one verb that knows retired config and `.env` names. It renames a retired `.env` key in place and lists the refused keys. The manager's four `enabled` switches default on.
- [#946](../../issues/946) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two switches, two names: `--dry-run` shows the plan on a verb that acts by default, `--execute` does the work on one that reports by default. A bare `omega update` installs the safe set; `--apply`, `--check` and the `outdated`/`out` aliases are gone.
- [#888](../../issues/888) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate`'s report ends with one line naming the breaking-changes register inside the installed manager, where every by-hand step for a shape OMEGA changed after a conversion lives.

### Removed
- [#990](../../issues/990) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Changesets is gone: the `.changeset/` folder, the `@changesets/cli` dev dependency and its config test. A release is bumped by hand in its `chore(release)` commit, and the lockstep check now fails when any publishable's version differs from the root's ([docs/shared/publishing.md](docs/shared/publishing.md)).
- [#994](../../issues/994) [`e01d67ee`](../../commit/e01d67ee) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - OMEGA writes no `CLAUDE.md` anywhere: a manage run no longer creates the `@AGENTS.md` pointer at the brand root, and the target scaffolds no longer copy one. Claude Code reads `AGENTS.md` itself; a `CLAUDE.md` you keep is left alone.
- [#1001](../../issues/1001) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Extension targets no longer get a `.vscode/settings.json`. The shipped one set nothing, and every run overwrote it whole. A copy already in your target is yours and stays untouched.

### Fixed
- [#1002](../../issues/1002) [`bc34c957`](../../commit/bc34c957) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every `.env` line the framework writes is `KEY="value"`, unescaped: empty is `KEY=""`, a placeholder `# KEY=""`. A value that would read back changed is refused naming the key, including in the staged `dist/.env` Firebase also reads. GitHub's runner `.env` stays bare.
- [#982](../../issues/982) [`a4fc00dc`](../../commit/a4fc00dc) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The devkit's child boot and the journey harness's server boot reject an early exit only after the child's pipes are read (bounded when a grandchild holds them) and the log tee has finished, so the reason it died is on disk before the lane reads the log.
- [#987](../../issues/987) [`f585065d`](../../commit/f585065d) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The dist audit reads a whole `<meta>` or `<img>` tag even when a quoted attribute holds a raw `>` (the minifier decodes `&gt;` there), so a description or title with angle brackets no longer hides the tag from the meta and alt checks.
- [#815](../../issues/815) [`b938afb1`](../../commit/b938afb1) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The dependency tree moved off 93 of the 121 open Dependabot findings: same-major bumps through the update verb (mjml, fast-xml-parser, sanitize-html, liquidjs, cheerio, js-yaml) and a lock refresh under the 7-day quarantine; the 28 that stay are pinned by upstream ranges or have no patched version.
- [#976](../../issues/976) [`b938afb1`](../../commit/b938afb1) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega i local` flips a registry pin whose tree already resolves to the monorepo copy, so a playground manifest left on a published version cannot let the next install shadow the local code.
- [#963](../../issues/963) [`b938afb1`](../../commit/b938afb1) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The two MCP admin-key checks (the OAuth admin path and the token classification) compare with the constant-time `safeCompare` the request pipeline uses, so a plain `===` no longer leaks the match length through timing.
- [#973](../../issues/973) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The monorepo's root `AGENTS.md` reads as the map again: its headings and blank lines are back and the desktop consumer template a wrong-directory `omega deploy` merged into it is gone. A refused workspace-root scaffold is test-pinned to leave the root's `AGENTS.md` and `.gitignore` untouched.
- [#970](../../issues/970) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate`'s web leg converts a UJM target into the brand root's `config/omega.json5`: an existing value wins, the report names each key kept and added, and web-only sections land under the target's folder name. It used to write a target-level config that overrode the brand root.
- [#862](../../issues/862) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega i live` prunes the brand's lockfile before its install and fails loudly if any `@omega.js` package is still a symlink afterwards. The install used to honor the old links and leave the tree linked into the checkout while reporting success.
- [#968](../../issues/968) [`0e330644`](../../commit/0e330644) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The six published manifests spell their bin paths as `bin/omega`, not `./bin/omega`, so `npm publish` stops warning that every bin entry was "invalid and removed" (npm kept them; the warning was wording).
- [#932](../../issues/932) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A deploy's workflow push deletes a composed workflow the brand no longer produces (a renamed or dropped target) from the repo's default branch, in the same `chore(ci)` commit; hand-written workflows are never touched. A retired file used to stay there, still dispatchable.
- [#943](../../issues/943) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega manage` materializes a target-only default, such as web's `meta.index` or extension's `categories`, under each declared `targets.<name>` that lacks it. The walk used to report every default present while those keys were never written.
- [#971](../../issues/971) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega manage --dry-run` writes nothing in the workspace service: the `.gitignore` entries, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/settings.json` and the framework-map link print as plan lines. A dry run used to write all of them.
- [#944](../../issues/944) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web scaffold, the desktop, extension and backend defaults, the backend templates, the composed workflows and every committed file the manager scaffolds for a brand or company carry no em dash, so a consumer repo whose guard refuses one can commit them. Old dashed workflow headers are still swept.
- [#942](../../issues/942) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The comments `omega manage` writes above materialized config blocks read as plain present-tense notes on what each key does and its default. The schema descriptions they come from used to carry em dashes, issue tags and history into every brand's `omega.json5`.
- [#933](../../issues/933) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A backend `omega deploy --direct` stopped before its stage (a license refusal, say) takes its `dist/deploy.log` tee off with it, and the deploy test harnesses restore the log stack instead of popping blind, so an `omega test` run's own log keeps recording.
- [#941](../../issues/941) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The machine registry `~/.omega/brands.json` keeps every brand whose root is on disk: a write is one atomic rename, an unreadable file is never rewritten, and test fixtures record into a temp home. Racing writers used to leave it holding one line, dropping the company.
- [#935](../../issues/935) [`32dbe62c`](../../commit/32dbe62c) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension's background test lane attaches the live service worker for every suite and releases a restarted worker's start pause, so a suite fails naming `service worker for <id> is not active` instead of hanging or reading an undefined `chrome.runtime`.

## [0.53.0] - 2026-09-26

### Added
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `User` from `@omega.js/account` is one class on the backend and in the browser: the stored account as own fields, `authenticated`, `plan`, `active`, `trialing`, `cancelling` and `everPaid` as getters, `profile` from the sign-in. `ctx.user` and every `omega.auth.user` is one, never null.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega.auth.reload()` re-reads the account and lands a new `{ user, denied }` state. `omega.ready` is the promise `initialize()` settles, so a module that did not boot the instance can await it.

### Changed
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every package is consumed one way: the default export is the ready-made instance `omega`, `initialize()` returns it, and modules are properties (`omega.auth.user`, `omega.utilities.escapeHTML()`). Migration steps: `docs/shared/breaking-changes.md`.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every runtime entry is two lines, the instance and then `omega.initialize()`: browser entries (web, extension, the desktop renderer) `import omega from '...'`, and Node entries (backend, desktop main and preload) `require` it. A backend ends with `module.exports = omega.functions`.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Routes receive `({ ctx, omega, user, data, usage, analytics })`, events `({ ctx, omega, user, context, change, snapshot })`, cron jobs `({ ctx, omega, context })`. Bindings read one root, `auth.user` (`auth.user.plan`, `auth.user.authenticated`), and `auth.listen` delivers `{ user, denied }`.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop and extension build modules are plain (`const build = require('@omega.js/desktop/build'); build.getConfig()`), and every hook receives `{ build, projectRoot, mode }`.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension background and desktop main both carry `omega.request()` and `omega.auth.getIdToken()`. The desktop renderer carries the `.omega-signin` and `.omega-account` triggers, and its `.omega-signout` signs the whole app out through main.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Consumer routes leave `omega_api` and `/omega/*`: each gets its own function, hosting rewrite and path (`/notes`). `omega.routes.run(name, { req, res }, options)` and `omega.events.run(name, payload)` replace `omega.run` and `omega.runEvent`. An MCP tool's `path` is the path as served.
- [#823](../../issues/823) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One request-schema system: a schema file returns a plain field declaration (`{ type: 'string', required: true }`) from `({ user, body, query, path, method, headers, geolocation })`, and one zod adapter validates it. `pattern`, `path`, `of` and `fields` join the vocabulary.

### Removed
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Backend: the `_legacy` functions and `setupFunctionsLegacy`, `ApiManager`, `Roles`, `install()`, `debug()`, `self.interface`, `libraries.localDatabase`, `fetchStats`, `server-manager.js`, and the `/backend-manager/*` URL alias, which answers 404 now.
- [#945](../../issues/945) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Frontends: `omega.library()`, the client's `getUser()`, `isAuthenticated()` and `resolveSubscription()`, the `auth.account.*` and `auth.resolved.*` bindings, the extension's root export, `openAuthPage()` and `attachTo` mixins, desktop's `manager.omega`, `./lib/logger` and `__EM_TEST__`, and `@omega.js/config`'s `attachTo`.
- [#823](../../issues/823) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The hand-rolled schema engine and the zod builder layer (`f.object`, `f.string` and the rest): `helpers/schema.js` is the one adapter.

### Fixed
- [#950](../../issues/950) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The admin users page calls the snapshot's `exists()`, so viewing a user with no Firestore document says so instead of showing an empty `{ id }` record.
- [#951](../../issues/951) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend setup check for the staged functions manifest compares against the target manifest instead of reading an undefined `app`.
- [#965](../../issues/965) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend's `@omega.js/backend` version check reads the package the target resolves, passes on a monorepo checkout whatever the manifest pins, and only warns, naming `npx omega update`, instead of installing from the registry and exiting mid-run.
- [#961](../../issues/961) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A consumer handler MCP tool now receives the request `Context` as `ctx` and the resolved caller as `user`, instead of the logger and `null`. The MCP endpoint resolves its caller once, through `Context.authenticate()`.
- [#953](../../issues/953) [`b471c376`](../../commit/b471c376) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page with no translation (cold under `--cached-only`, or a provider failure) ships its source copy at the language path, unadvertised, so built sites and deploys carry no dead language links. `omega translate` fills them.

## [0.52.0] 2026-09-24

### Added
- [#881](../../issues/881) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The omega plugin refuses `npx omega|omg|mgr` (and `npm exec`) in an agent shell when no `node_modules/.bin/omega` resolves from the working directory up: with no local bin npx silently fetches a stranger's `omega` package and runs it with the shell's env.
- [#467](../../issues/467) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega audit` reports each page's LCP, CLS and the form factor the run emulated beside the four scores, and `--max-lcp=<ms>` gates on it: any page whose LCP is over the bar fails loudly and exits 1. Run `npx omega audit --max-lcp=1300` before a deploy.

### Changed
- [#937](../../issues/937) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npx omega runner start` is the one command a signing box needs: it installs a bare box, refreshes a stale runner download in place, registers any admin org the box does not serve yet, then brings every org online. `runner self-update` is gone; every verb runs from the clone.
- [#927](../../issues/927) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The companion extension leaves `@omega.js/manager`: it is the OMEGA Companion, the OMEGA brand's own extension target on the Chrome Web Store lane, and its MCP bridge ships in `@omega.js/mcp-router` at `servers/omega-extension/`, so the `omega-extension` upstream starts with nothing else installed.
- [#929](../../issues/929) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every section arg, page-layout arg and runtime builder emits an `icon` as the authored Font Awesome class string too, so a section can name a brand mark. The footer's social row and a member's link id stay platform keys, rendering the brand family they always meant.
- [#903](../../issues/903) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A link's `icon` key carries the full Font Awesome class string across the base theme's chrome: nav, sidebar, topbar, page header, account dropdown and account section header emit it verbatim, as the footer already did, so `icon: fa-brands fa-github` renders a brand mark. Prefix every bare name with `fa-solid`.
- [#920](../../issues/920) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - yargs leaves all five CLIs for `node:util.parseArgs`, through one devkit helper (`@omega.js/devkit/argv`) carrying yargs' own value rule: a CLI declares only its value-LESS flags, and every other flag takes the next token as its value. It rebuilds the camelCase twins, `--no-<x>` negation and repeatable flags too.
- [#877](../../issues/877) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The four `omega-<framework>` bins are gone: every framework ships `omega`, `omg` and `mgr` only, and a human types `omega`. No generated workflow runs `npx` at all now; each runs its framework's own `bin/omega` file by path, under the checkout root npm hoists it to.
- [#467](../../issues/467) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every visible lockup (nav, footer, sidebar, auth, app, checkout, portal, extension, download) renders a brand's minted `brandmark.svg` when it exists, else the configured raster; `brand.images.brandmark` stays raster for payment and social cards.

### Fixed
- [#934](../../issues/934) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A clone whose `origin` is not the repo the config derives stops `omega manage`, `omega deploy` and the secrets precheck before they touch it, dry runs included. The boot line compares the whole slug, so a renamed repo is caught as well as a transferred one.
- [#940](../../issues/940) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A failed extension store publish says why: the store table's row and the thrown error carry each store's reason, where both used to print only the store name. A version the store already holds reads `version 0.0.4 already exists on Firefox Add-ons. Bump version in package.json and deploy again.`
- [#939](../../issues/939) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop workflow keeps its `windows-unsigned` artifact one day instead of GitHub's 90: `windows-sign` consumes it in the same run and the releases repo holds the real assets, so the stale copies filled the org's artifact storage quota until a Windows build failed at upload.
- [#938](../../issues/938) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A registry deploy ships a lockfile that matches the manifests. `omega i live` and `omega i local` regenerate the brand's own lockfile through one devkit helper, and the unlinked snapshot lane refuses a stale lock before anything is pushed, dry runs included. Every framework workflow installs with `sfw npm ci`.
- [#921](../../issues/921) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A duplicate launch's real command line reaches the running desktop app: the single-instance lock passes `{ argv, cwd }`, which arrives untouched as `second-instance`'s `additionalData`, and deep-link extraction reads it. The event's own argv is Chromium-processed (switches first, values detached) and only the fallback.
- [#916](../../issues/916) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The wordmark never ships a sliver: the assets service renders its own SVG path data, because opentype.js 2.0.0 rounds a float-noise coordinate to NaN and truncates the glyph there.
