A path still carrying NaN or Infinity fails the run, naming the font file and the text.
- [#908](../../issues/908) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - With no target context the `omega` bin runs an INSTALLED `@omega.js/manager` before any host fallback. A fresh brand-template clone that installed only the manager ran the backend's CLI, since the manager depends on it and npm's hoist linked its bin, so `npx omega onboard` answered `Unknown command`.
- [#907](../../issues/907) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A test run never asks the OS keychain: auth persistence is `none` on every layer, before any config read and whatever the brand declares. The boot lane gained a 60s budget (`OMEGA_TEST_BOOT_TIMEOUT_MS`): it fails the run with the last `runtime.log` line and ends the child, instead of hanging.
- [#906](../../issues/906) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A CommonJS bundle keeps `import.meta.url`: desktop's main and preload no longer hand an ESM-only dependency `undefined`, so `createRequire(import.meta.url)` stops throwing at boot. One rule in devkit's `bundle()`, and every target proves an ESM-only package loads through its real lane.
- [#467](../../issues/467) [`cfc83da0`](../../commit/cfc83da0) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The head warms the analytics and Firebase origins a page really uses, each gated on the config its loader reads; the theme's italic latin faces preload too, ending the 0.02 shift the hero accent caused; and every page whose opening band is a masthead paints as a first-paint band.

## [0.51.0] 2026-09-15

### Added
- [#875](../../issues/875) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The self-hosted Windows signer refuses any job that is not a trusted dispatch: a job-started hook checks the event, repository and actor against allow lists `omega runner install` writes beside the runner. The generated sign job skips a non-dispatch trigger, and the manage walk warns on one.
- [#901](../../issues/901) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand-root `omega deploy` scaffolds every selected target in-process before its one snapshot push, so each target's composed workflow rides that snapshot. Every framework exposes its `ensureTarget` at one subpath, `@omega.js/<framework>/ensure-target`, which the manager's `resolveTargetScaffold` resolves and calls: no verb and no flag, a deploy always scaffolds first.
- [#867](../../issues/867) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One shipping declaration on both targets, `targets.<name>.platforms.<platform>.formats.<format>`: presence enables, `false` drops, per-format settings live inside. @omega.js/config's `platforms.js` is the format table every reader derives from, and each format names the keys it cannot ship without.
- [#867](../../issues/867) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Env schema entries carry `label`, `url` (the page that mints the value) and `hint`, the one home for what a key is and where it comes from. Every desktop signing key and extension store key has one now, so the walk can ask for them.
- [#867](../../issues/867) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `publishing` service asks for every developer key and store listing id the brand's declared formats need, derived from the format table. Publish refuses a declared store with no key, naming it and the walk, and prints one manual step for a listing nobody has created yet.
- [#890](../../issues/890) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A devkit boot prelude list runs right after the freshness guard on all five CLIs, each prelude targeting one verb, a list or all of them. First prelude: `origin` is healed onto the address GitHub's redirect answers, and an owner differing from `repo.org` prints one drift line.
- [#25](../../issues/25) [`b58c07f5`](../../commit/b58c07f5) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The seven publishables are on the npm registry at 0.50.0, the monorepo's own number: client, backend, mcp-router, extension, desktop, web and manager, each declaring `publishConfig.access: public`. A fresh install resolves them with no overrides; the first cut, 0.1.0, is deprecated.
- [#899](../../issues/899) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega deploy` on desktop runs the target's `hooks/deploy/pre.js` after the local scaffold and before its precheck, on both lanes; a dry run skips it, because a hook may act on the world.
- [#900](../../issues/900) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega deploy` on extension runs the target's `hooks/deploy/pre.js` too, through the package task's hook loader, with a scaffolded no-op default. The playground's shared lane keeps the two newest releases per target; the version comes from `omega bump` at the brand root.
- [#869](../../issues/869) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega bump patch|minor|major` at a brand root writes one version into the root and every target (plus the brand lock), and never commits. Every framework's `omega deploy` refuses a target whose version drifted from the root's, before its precheck.
- [#873](../../issues/873) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every dispatched deploy follows its run: devkit's one follower streams each job's log with its name in front, banners each transition, and the verb exits on the run's conclusion. The whole verb tees `logs/deploy.log` on all four targets.
- [#884](../../issues/884) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A FIRST Firefox publish carries AMO listing metadata: `web-ext sign --amo-metadata` with the brand description as the summary (cut to 250), `targets.<name>.categories` (AMO slugs, default `alerts-updates`) and the package license mapped to an AMO id. Every update carries none.
- [#856](../../issues/856) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every config layer takes an optional `config/omega.<environment>.json5` beside its `omega.json5`, the mirror of the `.env.<environment>` pair: overrides only, winning over its own base and losing to the layer above. Nothing scaffolds one. Every production lane names the environment it builds FOR; a dev boot takes the running one.

### Changed
- [#917](../../issues/917) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Docs, comments and code strings added by the deploy and config batch use commas, colons, parentheses or a new sentence where they carried em dashes; the CHANGELOG separator is the one exemption.
- (no issue) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - CHANGELOG entries separate the links from the text with a spaced hyphen instead of an em dash, history included, per the workkit entry format.
- [#926](../../issues/926) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The merged `Default Values` block of a `.gitignore` or `AGENTS.md` is exactly the new framework block: a line the framework retires now leaves the consumer instead of moving under the Custom marker. A `.env` key holding a real value still migrates; an empty one drops.
- [#923](../../issues/923) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `repository_dispatch` leaves all four workflow templates and the runner's job guard: an event type is not a ref, so such a run built the default branch, which never carries the deploy tree. `workflow_dispatch` at ref `omega-deploy` is the one way in.
- [#912](../../issues/912) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop and extension take their accent from `brand.color`: each sass task generates a `_brand.scss` holding `$primary` and the runtime `--omega-accent` ramp, from the one renderer in `@omega.js/devkit/brand-tokens` that web's inline ramp already used. The scaffolded literal is gone.
- [#897](../../issues/897) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand secrets folder has ONE spelling: `SECRETS_DIR`, `SERVICE_ACCOUNT_REL` and `GOOGLE_OAUTH_REL` in `@omega.js/devkit/service-account`. Every manager and backend reader of those two files imports them, and a devkit guard fails any package that builds the path by hand.
- [#915](../../issues/915) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - ONE deploy lane: every brand force-pushes its folder to `omega-deploy`, the only branch CI builds. The push lane, `--no-sync` and the `git add -A` commit of the developer's tree are retired. A default branch receives the composed workflow files alone, in a `chore(ci): compose` commit; a checkout behind it refuses.
- [#919](../../issues/919) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every admin publish write (the create's tree commit, the post edit, the content route) lands on the default branch AND `omega-deploy`, and the dispatch names that branch, so a brand on local framework packages builds what the last deploy pushed. No deploy branch yet: `deployDispatched: false`, never main.
- [#858](../../issues/858) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The same-name sweep's last two pairs: page `search.include`/`search.category` become `catalog.*` (the page's `pages.json` entry; config `search` stays Search Console), and `translation.exclude` becomes a `translation.include` glob list defaulting to `['**', '!blog/**']`, overridable per page under the same name. `omega migrate` converts the old list.
- [#882](../../issues/882) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `cloud.shared: true` skips the backend deploy: the verb quits at its first gate with one line and exit 0 on every lane, laptop, brand-root fan-out and the composed workflow's `--direct` step alike. A shared project is several brands' project, so no brand deploys into it.
- [#898](../../issues/898) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Each target's workflow job installs only its own workspace. Every template carries one `{{ installWorkspace }}` token, rendered into `--workspace .` on the brand-root copy and into nothing standalone, so no target's `engines.node` pin is installed under another job's Node.
- [#817](../../issues/817) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One environment module for every target, in `@omega.js/config`. It reads ONE input, `OMEGA_ENVIRONMENT` on Node and the baked `config.environment` in a browser, and throws when a context has neither. The four framework copies and backend's `environment()` alias are gone; the client gains `getEnvironment()`, `isProduction()` and `isTesting()`.
- [#817](../../issues/817) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The client's error reporter and service worker read the environment through that one surface, and `@omega.js/monitoring`'s production gate reads it too: `OMEGA_BUILD_MODE` no longer decides whether a host reports.
- [#834](../../issues/834) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The classic dev ports are defined once, in `@omega.js/config`. Every surface bakes that map as the floor of its `dev` block, so browser code reads a complete map instead of its own copy of the numbers. The nine literal fallbacks are gone, and a missing map throws.
- [#834](../../issues/834) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend reads those numbers from the same map: its emulator-config defaults and the `|| 5001`, `|| 5002`, `|| 4000` in its URL getters are gone, replaced by one dev-port read (the published `OMEGA_*_PORT` map, then `CLASSIC_PORTS`).
- [#847](../../issues/847) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One dispatch address helper in devkit, `dispatchTarget({ projectRoot, config, workflow })`: the repo the brand config names plus the workflow its scaffold composed. Desktop's local copy and the inline compositions in web, extension and backend are gone.
- [#867](../../issues/867) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One platform vocabulary everywhere OMEGA speaks for itself: `platforms.win` becomes `platforms.windows`, `config/icons/macos/` becomes `mac/`, the extension's `chromium` build becomes `chrome`, and download links become `/download/<platform>/<format>`. Run `omega manage --migration=platform-names --execute`; the old link segments redirect.
- [#867](../../issues/867) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manager's REQUIRES registry keeps only its own fields: every ask fills its label, mint page and hint from the env schema instead of a second copy per service. The `opera` extension build is gone, since no store ever published it.
- [#894](../../issues/894) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One browser subset and one `OMEGA_BUILD_JSON` on web, desktop and extension: a per-section `client` flag decides what reaches a browser, `clientConfig()` builds it, and `mode` is `{ environment, build, publish }` everywhere. Web's `Configuration` global retires with no alias.
- [#743](../../issues/743) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension's snapshot rides the shared wrapper: its hand-written allow list is gone, so a store artifact carries the schema-declared subset and `{ config, package, mode, license, builtAt }`, the same shape desktop writes.
- [#886](../../issues/886) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - BREAKING: every `targets` key is a NAME, `type` is written on every entry, and the web target's folder is `targets/web`. One `targetPath(config, name)` replaces the instance helpers. By hand: `git mv targets/website targets/web`, add `type` to every entry, turn any array into sibling keys.
- [#883](../../issues/883) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One `repo: { provider, org }` block, with every repo name derived by role: `<brand.id>-omega` for the source, `<brand.id>-releases` for artifacts, and `<brand.id>-<target>` per web target, so a private brand still serves Pages. Every override key is gone.
- [#887](../../issues/887) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend CMS routes commit INSIDE the web target: the path is `targetPath(config, target)` plus the site-relative one, and a `target` parameter names which web target, optional for a brand with one and required with several.
- [#893](../../issues/893) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Public identifiers live in config, not `.env`: the three store listing ids move into `targets.<name>.listings.<browser>.id`, the reCAPTCHA site key, PayPal client id and Chargebee site read from config, and a `.env` still declaring one of the six fails the load naming its new home.
- [#893](../../issues/893) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Firefox add-on id is ours, not the store's: the local scaffold pins the derived `extension@<brand host>` into the brand config as `listings.firefox.id` before any push, and a publish writes no config at all.
- [#677](../../issues/677) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One key joins a brand to its company: `company: { id, webhooks }`, which the loader fills from the parent's own config. Its config, `.env`, hooks and signing tree resolve from a `company/` folder `omega company init` creates there. Retires the top-level `parent` key and the company workspace.
- [#880](../../issues/880) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop release workflow matches its three siblings: both dispatch triggers, the shared header, one action-version pair (`actions/checkout@v7`, `actions/setup-node@v7`) across all four templates, shallow checkouts, a version log, and a `timeout-minutes` on every job.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every workflow template and every workflow in this repo moves to the current action majors: `actions/checkout@v7`, `actions/setup-node@v7`, `actions/upload-artifact@v7`, `actions/download-artifact@v8`, `actions/cache@v6` and `actions/setup-java@v6`. The family pin now covers all five `actions/*` the four templates use.
- [#872](../../issues/872) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One deploy lane on every target: `omega deploy` packs a linked brand's frameworks into tarballs, snapshots the brand folder to its repo and dispatches; `--direct` deploys here instead. Backend gains a workflow and a secrets precheck; the manage walk checks the org runner group.
- [#871](../../issues/871) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every scaffolded CI workflow gets Socket Firewall from Socket's own pinned GitHub Action, which downloads with the job's token and caches the binary, so a hosted runner's anonymous API quota no longer fails the install. The composer renders that step from one template token, once.
- [#872](../../issues/872) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The env schema gains `OMEGA_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON` (a backend `ci` delivery) and delivers `OMEGA_LICENSE_KEY` to the backend runner; the repo ensure and the deploy wait both flip a `gh-pages` default branch back to `main`; a runner whose HTTP functions cannot be made public fails the deploy by name.
- [#868](../../issues/868) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The prepare guard is required by a relative path and skips a checkout with no `node_modules`, so a linked brand's install runs it before there is anything to resolve or build with.
- [#808](../../issues/808) [`79dce108`](../../commit/79dce108) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The playground is id `playground` in `brands/playground-omega`; the real brand's repo and folder are `omega-omega`. One repo rule, applied everywhere.
- [#743](../../issues/743) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One `build.js` on every browser surface: each artifact writes `self.OMEGA_BUILD_JSON` to its web root, every HTML shell loads it first and every worker importScripts it. The bundle banners, the browser defines and web's inline foot script retire; desktop main and preload keep their own.
- [#896](../../issues/896) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The legacy `omega` build fact is gone: readers take `environment`, `buildTime` and `dev.liveReloadPort`, the names every surface spells. Desktop bakes `runtime: 'electron'`, so a packaged renderer stops reporting itself as `web`, and the web service worker reads the one client snapshot.
- [#895](../../issues/895) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega deploy --dry-run` RUNS the precheck on all four targets and sends nothing: the plan names the secret keys it would publish, the derived signing lines and any refusal, so a deploy can be previewed without publishing it.
- [#901](../../issues/901) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand-root `omega deploy` runs the lane's delivery ONCE and hands every target the snapshot sha, then runs the backend alone and web, desktop and extension together, each group line prefixed with its target.
- [#819](../../issues/819) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every env key says how it reaches each target it names, held by a schema test: `OMEGA_ADMIN_KEY` names the backend alone, the two test-lane keys retire for a seeded emulator persona ([#904](../../issues/904)), and `OPENAI_API_KEY` reaches no runner, since CI reads the committed translation cache ([#905](../../issues/905)).
- [#835](../../issues/835) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A key of your OWN in the brand `.env` or its `.env.production` now reaches GitHub Actions: one delivery primitive reads the schema plus the target's composed production values, and a custom key travels in its target's file mode. Machine-local and local-only keys are the one skip.

### Fixed
- [#925](../../issues/925) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/client` treats a testing run handed a dev port map as a local stack: it connects the Auth and Firestore SDKs to the emulators and routes the API locally, so a boot-lane renderer answering `testing` signs in against the emulator instead of live Firebase.
- [#873](../../issues/873) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A verb run inside another verb's process (the deploy tests inside `omega test`) stacks its log tee on the outer one instead of replacing it, so the outer log keeps receiving after the inner verb ends.
- [#924](../../issues/924) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two backend tests (the environment helper, the CMS target paths) build their fixtures in a temp dir instead of requiring a vendored package or a `brands/` path, so the suite-portability check passes on a dev tree, a local install and a published install alike.
- [#928](../../issues/928) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The JSON5 defaults merge stops re-stringifying a project's `config/omega.json5`: only the differing paths go through `@omega.js/config`'s comment-preserving editor, so comments and blank lines survive, a written file ends with one newline, and an unchanged merge writes nothing.
- [#925](../../issues/925) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `OMEGA_ENVIRONMENT` is the one input on desktop: the word a build baked is the fallback for a packaged app, never an override, so a boot-lane app answers `testing` again and its renderer takes the running word from the preload. The runners' stale `OMEGA_TEST_MODE` and auth persistence's second signal are gone.
- [#922](../../issues/922) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The gh-pages default-branch heal runs in the deploy lane's first step, on the branch name it already read: a `gh-pages` default moves back to `main` (created from gh-pages when missing) before any workflow file is written there. Unreachable since [#915](../../issues/915) put every snapshot on `omega-deploy`.
- [#914](../../issues/914) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manager's companion extension is refreshed by its own scaffold: one ensure run regenerates the publish workflow it had drifted out of (dispatch-only, the pinned Socket action, its framework's own bin) plus the verb scripts, and a manager test re-runs that scaffold against the committed tree.
- [#913](../../issues/913) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A desktop target's `.gitignore` ignores `config/certs/*` and re-includes that directory's README, the one tracked explanation of where signing material really lives. A target scaffolded earlier converges on its next verb run: the old directory line, the retired sibling `.gitignore` and a stale README all heal.
- [#918](../../issues/918) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows signer prints its proof in the mac lane's shape once `signtool verify /pa` passes: `Signed and verified: signtool verify /pa accepts <file>.` A deploy log now shows the signature was CHECKED, where the old `Signed:` line only claimed one. Verify-only mode is unchanged.
- [#909](../../issues/909) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One bundle-id derivation, and it lives in `@omega.js/config`: a desktop `app.appId` defaults to the Apple `bundleIdPrefix` plus `brand.id` with dashes as dots, the very id the certificates service registers. The brand-URL host stays the fallback for a brand declaring no prefix.
- [#910](../../issues/910) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A company brand is sent to the right file: every preflight fix line names that company's `company/.env` by path, brand `.env` as the override. The workspace `env-rules` op derives `CSC_LINK` and `APPLE_API_KEY` from the signing tree before warning, as the secrets op does.
- [#911](../../issues/911) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The schema declares what the desktop runtime reads: `app.*`, `omega.authPersistence`, the `autoUpdate` block, `startup.openAtLogin.*`, `remoteConfig.*`, plus `type` on every target. `omega build`'s audit prints the validator's warnings and counts them; the dead `autoUpdate.channel` default is gone.
- [#870](../../issues/870) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A failing prepare no longer strips a package's #350 install gate: `rewire()` runs as the FIRST after hook (the watch lane too) and the prepare script restores the gate in a `finally`. The freshness heal verifies the manifest it prepared.
- [#845](../../issues/845) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The retired env register covers a key that only changed NAME: a layer carrying `OAUTH2_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` or `OAUTH2_GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` fails the load naming its `CONNECTIONS_*` replacement, instead of the brand's Google connection failing later as an absent credential.
- [#865](../../issues/865) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega deploy --direct` on the extension runs the one command CI runs, `npm run build` with the publish flag in its env: the old `release` script entered the publish task twice, publishing each version to every store twice over. That script is gone.
- [#865](../../issues/865) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop's `--direct` is pinned to the publish its CI runs: a test reads the `release:local` command out of the scaffolded `build.yml`, expands it through the target's scripts and runs the verb, so the workflow step, the script and `omega publish --local` cannot drift apart unseen.
- [#892](../../issues/892) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The certificates walk reuses signing material instead of minting beside it: every read looks in the company tree first, a valid cert pairs with a local key by modulus, and one expiry function reports days left, warning under 30 and erroring when expired.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A desktop release that uploaded NOTHING now fails the task: electron-builder resolves the same promise either way, so the task reads its logger for `skipped publishing` and names the reason, the tag and the version bump that fixes it.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The mac signing rungs of `validate-certs` run on the mac leg alone: the linux and windows legs blank those credentials on purpose, so checking them there only printed two warnings nobody could act on.
- [#873](../../issues/873) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The release follower renders a skipped job as ⊘ rather than ✗, so the legs a `platforms` input left out, and the Windows sign job with them, stop reading as failures beside a green run.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The mac DMG is signed with the Developer ID identity, then notarized, stapled and proved PER ARTIFACT, before electron-builder uploads it: the after-all hook ran after the upload was queued, so the published image carried no staple. The notarization runner reports a refusing tool's own verdict.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop floor for electron-builder is 26.16.1: every earlier build hands the `.p12` password to `set-key-partition-list`, which the macos-26 runner rejects, so a `CSC_LINK` signing leg died at import. ensure-target installs the floor for any consumer below it.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Keychain identity rung is read only when `CSC_LINK` names no certificate: electron-builder imports that `.p12` into its own temporary keychain, so a runner whose file rungs both passed no longer stops on it. The secrets lines print plain, not dim.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every rung of the mac signing ladder fails loudly on one answer: `CSC_LINK` and `APPLE_API_KEY` derive ONCE at the desktop env load, from the signing tree read in place. Retires the disperse certs copy, the `push-secrets` verb and its four binds; the secrets push is fatal everywhere.
- [#879](../../issues/879) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manage walk's runner-group check reads a `visibility: selected` group's repository list, so a group that allows public repositories but leaves the brand repo off it fails the walk by name instead of passing green while a dispatched desktop release queues forever.
- [#878](../../issues/878) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manage walk grants the deploy service account `roles/iam.serviceAccountUser` on the App Engine and compute default service accounts, so a dispatched backend deploy can act as them; the deploy precheck reads both policies first and names a missing grant with the exact gcloud command.
- [#872](../../issues/872) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop CI builds run for a brand with no certificate: the firewall binary works under cmd, inline base64 credentials are accepted, mac assets decode only when present, unsigned apps skip notarization, the `.deb` gets homepage and maintainer from the brand, and every template runs its framework bin with `npx --no-install`.
- [#866](../../issues/866) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The packaged desktop app no longer carries the target's scratch and state dirs: `.omega`, `.claude`, `.temp`, `.cache`, `.gh-runners` and `test` are excluded, so a stale boot-test symlink can no longer break a release.
- [#864](../../issues/864) [`a694edd5`](../../commit/a694edd5) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Windows sign at a locked console no longer hangs six hours: `sign-windows` refuses a locked console up front, each `signtool` attempt has a three-minute limit, the PIN watcher reports a dialog it could not reach, and the sign job carries `timeout-minutes`.
- [#876](../../issues/876) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A dispatched backend deploy no longer drops the `CONNECTIONS_*` OAuth family: the schema knows it only as a pattern, so the composed production env expands it at push time, one Actions secret per key, and the workflow's `.env` writer names that same set.
- [#902](../../issues/902) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A snapshot deploy waits until GitHub's ref resolves to the sha it pushed, and refuses a run whose `head_sha` is another tree, naming both shas and the run URL. A dispatch sent in the same second as the force-push built the PREVIOUS snapshot, republishing the version before this one, green.

### Removed
- [#850](../../issues/850) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The four schema-less web sections `targets.web.{favicon,manifest,icons,currency}`: each is a retired path now, the favicon set is minted with no override, `theme-color` comes from `brand.color`, a footer link carries its own `fa-*` classes, and the pricing JSON-LD reads `payment.currency`.
- [#891](../../issues/891) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `You are using the local version` warning on desktop and the extension: the deploy snapshot packs every locally linked package into the mirror it pushes, so a local install is exactly what the deploy is built for.
- [#872](../../issues/872) [`575066df`](../../commit/575066df) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `rehearse:desktop` script, the two `scripts/playground-desktop-*.js` generators and the private rehearsal lane; and the 2026-07-20 rule that a linked brand deployed from the local machine on its own (`--direct` is the explicit ask now).

## [0.50.0] 2026-09-09

### Added
- [#813](../../issues/813) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A renderer suite reaches the project's own views: `view: '<name>'` runs it on the boot lane's built app, so the page carries the real preload, the real IPC handlers and the real config. The playground's main-view assertions moved there from its boot suite.
- [#811](../../issues/811) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The playground desktop target ships tests of its own: a build suite pinning the identity its `omega.json5` resolves (brand, product name, both derived repos) and a boot suite launching the real bundle to check the window, the three integrations and the main view.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Before every run the rehearsal publishes the playground desktop target's composed secrets (the same set `omega push-secrets` composes for the brand, file-valued keys base64) to the private rehearsal repo, because push-secrets targets only the brand's declared repo and skips the snapshot repo as a remote mismatch.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One command rehearses the playground desktop run: `npm run rehearse:desktop -- --platforms windows [--watch]` renders the workflow, ensures the private repo, publishes the secrets, snapshots the working tree through a temporary index onto `refs/rehearsal/head` (no local branch, `main` untouched), force-pushes it to that repo's `main` and dispatches.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The playground desktop release run has a repo of its own, private and throwaway (`Omega-JS-Stack/omega-playground-rehearsal`), because a public snapshot would publish the framework source. Its workflow, rendered from the desktop template through `composeWorkflow`, is gitignored here and lives only in that snapshot repo.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The orgs checkbox in `omega runner install` and `config` lists orgs alphabetically (case-insensitive) instead of GitHub's membership order.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega runner install` and `config` are ONE walk: every key of the box `.env` asked with its current value as the default, secrets never echoed, every answer written back, then an orgs checkbox ticked to the saved list, else the registered orgs, else nothing. `start` asks only for missing keys.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every `omega runner` subcommand and `omega sign-windows` append their output to `<runner home>\logs\runner.log`, during CI jobs too (devkit's tee gains `append` and `attachInCI`); `runner status` prints the path. Off Windows a subcommand refuses before it writes anything.
- [#784](../../issues/784) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `action=authorize` takes an optional `returnUrl`: a path on this site (one leading `/`, no scheme, `//`, backslash or whitespace, else 400) that rides the encrypted state and comes back out of `tokenize`. The callback page lands there instead of `/dashboard/account#connections`, re-checking the rule before it navigates.
- [#779](../../issues/779) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An mcp-router upstream's child closes after 15 minutes with no call (`MCP_ROUTER_IDLE_MS` is the seam), and closing takes the whole process TREE, on shutdown and when the host's stdin closes. A call landing mid-close gets a fresh child, and `router__list_upstreams` carries each child's `pid` and `idle_ms`.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand gets its own browser lane: `omega test` at a brand root walks `<brandRoot>/test/e2e/run.js` after every target, and skips a folder with no entry in one line.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega test --target=web,backend` narrows project, framework and `full:` runs alike. A framework scope whose target the picker excludes refuses by name instead of testing nothing.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `--extended` fans out to every target, and `--lane=<name>` to the frameworks that declare it in their package.json `omega.testLanes`. A target serving no such lane prints one line and stays green.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Devkit exports the e2e lane pieces every lane now shares: `test/boot-child` (child boot on a ready marker, group stop), `test/port-hold` (hold the classic ports so the stack bumps past a live dev boot), `test/browser` (puppeteer resolved from the brand root).
- [#81](../../issues/81) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two CLI parity gaps closed: backend's `install` and `update` answer `-i` / `-u` / `--install` / `--update` like every sibling, and extension gains `omega build` - clean, then the gulp build under `OMEGA_BUILD_MODE`, the pipeline the consumer's `build` script now aliases.
- [#774](../../issues/774) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every backend email is provable offline: outside extended mode `send()` records what it would have handed SendGrid - recipients, template, subject, rendered text - to `.temp/test-emails.jsonl`. The per-caller `isTesting()` gates in signup, account delete and the dispute handler are gone; extended mode still sends real mail.
- [#773](../../issues/773) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An `omega:theme` skill routing the theme cascade's rules, which the gate now fires on theme surfaces alongside that surface's own skill; plus a `shape-theme-override` corpus cell proving the tier-2 cascade - layout, include, scss entry, section html and js - in a real build.
- [#149](../../issues/149) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `docs/manager/migration.md`, the playbook for taking a legacy brand onto OMEGA: six phases with checkable exit criteria, per-target checklists for website/backend/desktop/extension, and a trap register of silent-failure classes. Every real-brand run is hard gated on Ian's go.
- [#771](../../issues/771) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand adds any connection provider with one file: `targets/backend/src/connections/<name>.js` loads before the framework's own, `pkce: 'S256'` is the whole PKCE declaration, `authorize`/`exchange`/`refresh` override the defaults, and the account page's cards follow the `connections` config instead of an allowlist. Twitch and Kick ship built in.
- [#452](../../issues/452) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `omega:guard` hook refuses a write to a framework-owned file in a brand: generated and vendored files name their real source, and a copy of the override layer names the path it shadows. Pages and documented entry files stay free; `omega customize` writes the `omega:consumer-override:` marker.
- [#770](../../issues/770) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega onboard` asks for the contact PERSON who signs the welcome, nudge and checkup emails, with a skippable headshot and link URL, and writes `brand.contact.person`. `--contactName` (plus `--contactImage`/`--contactUrl`) answers it non-interactively; without it nothing is written, since a human name is never invented.
- [#760](../../issues/760) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The omega:gate hook gains a second lane for agents with no Skill tool: `agent-plugins/claude/hooks/gate/mark.sh <skill>` writes the same session marker the Skill event writes, taking the session id from `--session` or the environment. The gate stays a main-chat guard, and a hand-written marker is a breach.
- [#768](../../issues/768) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Body text no longer reflows when a webfont lands: the web build measures each vendored family's font file and generates a metric-matched `<Family> Fallback` face (`size-adjust` plus the vertical overrides), named in every `--omega-font-*` stack. Free for any theme, no config key.
- [#642](../../issues/642) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Crypto checkout works: a Coinbase Commerce provider (hosted charge, `hosted_url` redirect, `charge:confirmed`/`pending`/`failed` webhooks on the one `?key=` check) and the crypto pay button back, shown only when `payment.providers.coinbase.enabled` is set and the product is one-time. Key: `COINBASE_COMMERCE_API_KEY`, asked at manage time. Coinbase cannot refund, so a crypto order is refused.
- [#201](../../issues/201) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop's `npx omega logs` takes a surface: `npx omega logs [runtime|dev|build|test]` reads `<projectRoot>/logs/<surface>.log`, so `dev.log`, `build.log` and `test.log` get the same print/follow/path/open flags `runtime.log` always had. No positional still means `runtime`, and an unknown name fails naming the four.
- [#468](../../issues/468) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega test` adds four fast scans of the built site: a non-empty title and description per page, every in-site `#anchor` landing on a real id, an `alt` on every `<img>`, and no sitemap orphans. Opt out per page in `config/link-exceptions.json5`.
- [#338](../../issues/338) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop update simulator gains a runtime trigger: `manager.autoUpdater.simulate(scenario)`, wired to a dev-only Simulate update submenu under View → Developer. QA walks the available, unavailable and error outcomes without relaunching per scenario; a packaged production build refuses unless launched with `OMEGA_DEV_UPDATE` set.
- [#751](../../issues/751) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The edge defaults carry an HTML cache rule beside the asset one, so a brand declaring no `cacheRules` gets both lifetimes: `/assets` for a year, and HTML for one minute in the browser plus a purge-on-deploy 2 hours at the edge. The HTML rule matches site hosts only, never `api.<domain>`.
- [#663](../../issues/663) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page collects a **Street** and a **Postal code** beside country/region/city, stored as `personal.location.street` / `personal.location.postalCode`. Meta's `zp` rides every server conversion, and GA4's `postal_code` / `sha256_street` ride the authenticated requests its Measurement Protocol helper builds from - all three were empty on every account until now.
- [#40](../../issues/40) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npx omega migrate:markers` converts a legacy backend target's pre-family markers (`{{ backend-manager }}`, `# BEM>>>` gitignore blocks, the `///---...---///` rules flavors) to the family grammar. The setup checks defer on those shapes and name the verb; the compiler refuses to bake them into dist.
- [#621](../../issues/621) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Framework default pages (auth, account, checkout, portal) ship translated chrome: es and fa caches generated once framework-side, packaged in `@omega.js/web`, applied by the brand's translate pass at zero provider cost. Misses stay English with one tagged log line; legal pages stay one-language.
- [#653](../../issues/653) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Claude plugin loads every skill a brand needs - `omega:main`, `omega:manager`, and one per declared target, read from `config/omega.json5` and each `targets/*/package.json` - and ENFORCES it: a write under `targets/<t>/`, to `config/omega.json5`, or to a monorepo package is refused, naming the skill, until it has been invoked.
- [#603](../../issues/603) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Brand-root `omega build` and `omega clean` fan out over EVERY target, framework and custom, backend first: each framework's own verb or the target's matching script, a loud skip when there is none, and one run naming every broken target.
- [#603](../../issues/603) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.<name>: { type: 'custom' }` declares a target no framework owns; its own `package.json` scripts define `start`, `build`, `test`, `deploy`, `clean`. Framework services skip it - the workspace service does not.
- [#586](../../issues/586) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `.env.development` / `.env.testing` / `.env.production` overlay the `.env` beside them, at every layer of the cascade. The LANE names the environment, never the shell: deploy composes production, emulator/serve/mcp development, a test lane testing, the manage and devlog walks production.
- [#600](../../issues/600) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` on a website OR a backend target reports every bare `require()` under `src/**` the target's package.json never declares, at file:line, with the fix. Never installed; the web bundler's aliases are never named. One scan, in `@omega.js/devkit`.
- [#672](../../issues/672) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page carries an Orders section: every purchase, read through the new `GET /user/orders` (admin SDK server-side, Firestore rules untouched). The refund form posts the picked `orderId`, so the backend's guarded one-time refund lane is reachable at last.
- [#623](../../issues/623) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand placement contract: one table naming every surface as authored, walked, distributed or generated. Every brand-root verb tees to `logs/<verb>.log` - build, clean, deploy, update, test and pipeline join manage and dev - and an unchanged `google-oauth.json` is no longer rewritten each run.
- [#696](../../issues/696) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The cloud walk NAMES the OAuth consent branding page (logo, home/privacy/terms links, authorized domains), which has no API: the console URL plus one line - links and authorized domains are safe anytime, a logo upload starts Google's verification review for External apps.
- [#717](../../issues/717) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/web` answers `getEnvironment()` plus `isDevelopment()` / `isProduction()` / `isTesting()`: the same four calls, same semantics, as its backend, desktop and extension siblings, mixed into the CLI class via `attachTo()`. The build lanes read that surface instead of a loose `environment` string.
- [#681](../../issues/681) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web, extension and desktop dev lanes watch the whole `.env` chain, every layer with its `.env.<environment>` overlay, and reload it into `process.env`. `omega manage`'s build cache fingerprints the overlays too, so an overlay edit rebuilds the target.
- [#724](../../issues/724) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `reloadEnv()` re-reads the `.env` cascade so an EDITED value lands in `process.env`, not just a new key. Ownership is remembered, never inferred from presence: the keys the shell brought before the first file read always win, and a key dropped from the file is dropped from the process.
- [#725](../../issues/725) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.web.meta.index: false` takes a whole site out of search in one move: every page emits noindex and `sitemap.xml`, `llms.txt` and `pages.json` list nothing. It seeds the one `meta.index` flag, so a page's own `meta.index: true` still exempts it. Only the literal `false`.
- [#320](../../issues/320) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Published installs take an `OMEGA_LICENSE_KEY`: each target's production build asks omegajs.dev once and bakes the verdict. A valid key enables live payments and removes attribution; keyless ships attribution shown, payments gated. Dev and `demo-*` never check; a key that cannot verify fails the deploy loudly.
- [#620](../../issues/620) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop release assets carry versionless names from one config catalog, so `releases/latest/download/<asset>` is a stable link. The download page and `/download/<platform>/<artifact>` shortlinks point at files, never the releases page; releasing a version never touches the website. Linux offers debian and appimage.

### Changed
- [#564](../../issues/564) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - BREAKING: the site-wide search posture is `targets.web.meta.index`, the same name a page writes. `seo.index` and page `sitemap.include` are retired; sitemap.xml, pages.json, llms.txt and the robots tag read one engine-resolved `meta.index`.
- [#809](../../issues/809) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand's SOURCE repo derives as `<brand.id>-omega`, the `<brand.id>-<role>` rule beside `<brand.id>-releases`; the bare-id default is retired. A typed `repo.providers.github.repo` slug still overrides it, and a brand that relied on the old default declares its name or renames the repo.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop release workflow's Test job is gone: CI is dispatch-only and builds, the way web's and the extension's release workflows already work, and the suites are proven on the developer's machine and at the ship gate instead.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop workflow template's build job checks out with the workflow's own token instead of the release PAT, so a bad PAT fails at the release step with an API error rather than at checkout.
- [#799](../../issues/799) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop's release verbs read the repo from config, never a git remote: `omega release` and `deploy` dispatch the brand repo, and the publish block, repo provisioning and finalize-release address one public `<brand.id>-releases`. The dispatched workflow is `desktop-build.yml` in a brand, `build.yml` standalone.
- [#801](../../issues/801) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega runner start` brings EVERY registered org's runner online, detached and idempotent: an org already alive is named with its PID and skipped, and the terminal is never taken over. `omega runner restart` stops, waits, then starts; `install` ends the same all-orgs way.
- [#795](../../issues/795) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every `omega dev` leg spawns with `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` set to mkcert's root, so a brand's own Node process trusts the local certificate on the public `https` port; the emulator's and `serve`'s children take that variable in place of `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0`, warning and all.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The five real-signing cases of `desktop:build/sign-windows-e2e` are an `--extended` lane: they drive the real EV token, so a bare run skips them everywhere, the signing box included. Run `npx omega test --extended desktop:build/sign-windows-e2e` on the box.
- [#793](../../issues/793) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - BREAKING (folds [#791](../../issues/791), [#792](../../issues/792)): a connection provider is one context per step - `identity` (required), `authorize`, `exchange`, `refresh`, `revoke`, `status`, one `urls.token`. The route owns the already-connected check, so reconnecting your own account works, and the account page's cards come from the config alone.
- [#794](../../issues/794) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `@omega.js` family ships lockstep: one version for all seven publishables, exact pins instead of carets in a brand's manifests, and a boot check that refuses `omega manage` or `omega dev` on a drifted target, naming the fix (`omega update --apply` at the brand root).
- [#788](../../issues/788) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - BREAKING: the user-connection feature is `connections` everywhere - the record, the `/omega/user/connections` route, the config section, the `CONNECTIONS_<PROVIDER>_*` env pair, `src/connections/`, and the `/connections/callback` page. Each record carries `type: 'oauth2'`. Move documents with `npx omega manage --migration=users --execute`; rename the env keys and register the new redirect URI by hand.
- [#780](../../issues/780) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `--target=<a,b>` is the one target picker on every brand-root verb: `deploy`, `build`, `clean`, `dev` and `update` take it like `omega test` does. `--only` (which collided with `firebase deploy --only hosting`) and `--except` are retired, and passing either fails naming `--target=`.
- [#780](../../issues/780) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `--target=` token that names no target now stops the run on every one of those verbs, naming the token and the brand's real targets. `omega test --target=web,typo` used to warn and test the matched half.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand e2e harness boots the REAL local stack: the backend emulator with seeded personas plus the website's `omega dev`, on allocator ports behind a classic-port hold. The static build it used to serve was always a production build, so its pages never reached the emulator.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The user-flows lane (`scripts/e2e-flows.js`) runs on the shared devkit pieces instead of its own copies of child boot/stop, the port hold and the browser launch. One boot mechanism, two lanes.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The sandbox brand's e2e lane moved to `brands/sandbox-brand/test/e2e/`, where the brand-root walk finds it.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The sandbox brand's website is a real `@omega.js/web` consumer instead of a hand-rolled esbuild page, so tier 2a drives the product's own boot: its `/e2e` page module carries the lane's hooks off the client the framework boots.
- [#775](../../issues/775) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/manager` carries the `puppeteer` dependency, so a brand installs nothing for its e2e lane: every brand root already installs the manager.
- [#647](../../issues/647) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web pricing composer refuses the pre-#647 product `features` LIST outright: a product still carrying the retired display array throws, naming the product and the migration, instead of quietly composing cards with no bullets and an empty comparison matrix.
- [#772](../../issues/772) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web framework draws with TanStack Charts instead of Chart.js: the same `core/js/libs/charts.js` helpers now mount an SVG host into a `<div>` slot, with the token colors, the `--omega-chart-*` ramp and the no-animation rule intact. `chart.js` leaves the tree.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows runner drops its logon-credential machinery - `WIN_RUNNER_LOGON_ACCOUNT`, `WIN_RUNNER_LOGON_PASSWORD`, the DPAPI store and the `runner set-credentials` subcommand - since a Startup-folder runner has no service to hand an account to; the `node-windows` dependency goes with it.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows signing runner sheds its dead admin-elevation and watcher-service code, `signtool sign` retries a failed timestamp call three times (stopping early on a wrong PIN), `WIN_EV_TOKEN_PATH` is the ONE cert key (the `WIN_CSC_LINK` alias is gone), and the Windows-gated `build/sign-windows-e2e` suite signs a real exe on the box.
- [#647](../../issues/647) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - BREAKING: a top-level `features` catalog defines each feature once and a product names only its value, retiring `limits`, the product `features` array and `rateLimit`. Counting is one call, `ctx.usage.consume('<feature>')`: month plus the day's share, a 429 naming which hit, catalog-declared mirrors, per-user override credits.
- [#498](../../issues/498) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Commerce events reach Meta and TikTok in the shape each documents today: Meta's `custom_data` gains `contents` and `order_id`, TikTok's `properties` gains `content_ids` and `order_id`, `content_name` rides only one-product events, `num_items` counts units, and a renewal tells Meta `action_source: system_generated`.
- [#746](../../issues/746) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The frontmatter guide now names the two per-page shell switches (`theme.sidebar.enabled` and `theme.topbar.enabled`) under `config:`, with a pointer to the shell-chrome contract.
- [#767](../../issues/767) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Page and layout sheets under 8 KB ship as a `<style>` block where their `<link>` stood: no request, same cascade. Bigger sheets keep their hashed file and link, `omega dev` keeps every link, and a css manifest entry is now `{ inline }` or `{ href }`.
- [#765](../../issues/765) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Font preloads come from the compiled sheet's `@font-face` rules, not file names: a face preloads when it declares no `unicode-range` or one reaching basic latin, so any theme's faces preload with nothing to declare. The list moved to `assetManifest.fontPreloads`; templates looping `site.fontPreloads` must read the new key.
- [#763](../../issues/763) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The first-viewport band fades in again, started at parse time by an inline head starter that mirrors the engine's stagger. `data-omega-first-paint` now means started early, not rendered final, and a first-paint band fades on its own `--omega-speed-first-paint` token (300ms) behind a 40ms stagger.
- [#750](../../issues/750) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A site's main stylesheet no longer blocks the first paint: the css lane extracts its first-paint subset (tokens, shell chrome, nav, hero) and the head inlines it, then loads the full sheet through `rel=preload` with a `<noscript>` fallback. `omega dev` keeps the plain blocking link.
- [#748](../../issues/748) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Backend, desktop and extension write their target scripts as bare `omega <verb>`, matching web; the next verb's ensure pass rewrites every framework-owned key in place. A consumer's own script keys are untouched, and `npx omega` stays canonical for docs and the terminal.
- [#738](../../issues/738) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/extension` bundles with esbuild through the shared `bundle()` wrapper; webpack, `babel-loader` and `@babel/preset-env` are gone. Every entry is one self-contained iife with no chunks or license sidecars, and the syntax floor is the MV3 minimums (`chrome88, firefox91`). Consumers: the gulp task is now `bundle` (was `webpack`).
- [#737](../../issues/737) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/desktop` bundles with esbuild through the shared `bundle()` wrapper; webpack and its loaders are gone. The syntax floor is read from the Electron binary the consumer pinned, and `--quick` now means only the trimmed electron-builder phase. The `em.webpack.externals` key is removed and the gulp task is now `bundle`.
- [#736](../../issues/736) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every framework bundles through ONE esbuild wrapper, `@omega.js/devkit`'s `bundle`: it composes the framework-dependency resolve hook, the production `@dev-only` strip and the minify/sourcemap rules, so a framework's own build code is just its entries and its plugins. `@omega.js/web` is on it first, and dev builds now emit sourcemaps.
- [#588](../../issues/588) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A web instance's `id` IS its subdomain: `web: [{ id: 'main' }, { id: 'admin' }]` serves `admin.<brand host>`, deploying to its own host and CNAME. An entry's own `url` still overrides it, every instance shares ONE `api.<domain>` and authDomain, and `subdomains` is retired.
- [#689](../../issues/689) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Standard target scripts are framework-owned: the manager walk and every framework's own scaffold rewrite them to the defaults on each run, consumer-added keys untouched. Backend joins its siblings in syncing them, and a custom-server backend keeps only the scripts whose verbs its mode refuses.
- [#619](../../issues/619) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One icon system: native Font Awesome markup everywhere (`<i class="fa-solid fa-user">`, flags as `omega-flag-us`). The build inlines every icon a page renders; a runtime watcher fetches whatever JS creates, from `/assets/icons/`. The `omega_icon` tag and `prerender_icons` are gone - `omega migrate` converts them.
- [#624](../../issues/624) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One rule for page assets, JS and CSS alike: every layer's file loads, in layer order (core → theme → consumer), so a theme page script no longer replaces core's. Layout-keyed assets (`js/layouts/<layout>.js`, `css/layouts/<layout>.scss`) replace the deleted `js/modules/` lane; `main.js` extends with `omega:main`.
- [#466](../../issues/466) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Redirects split by whether their URLs can be enumerated: a redirect PAGE now emits meta-refresh, a canonical to the DESTINATION and noindex, and a TEMPLATED one is `edge.providers.cloudflare.rules.redirect`. `targets.web.redirects`, its 404 answering and its dev mirror are gone; the key is a retired-key error.
- [#564](../../issues/564) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The robots meta and `sitemap.xml` are one decision: `meta.index: false` drives both. The blog taxonomy, a declared collection's category pages and every listing's `/page/N` are noindex and unlisted; posts, authored pages, collection items and each listing's page 1 stay indexed and listed. `omega migrate` names the `<loc>` delta.
- [#607](../../issues/607) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page overrides omega.json5 under a `config:` parent; `resolved.config.*` is the whole merged config, `resolved.meta.*` the page → layout walk, `site.*` build facts. A bare config section, or a non-section key under `config:`, fails the build. The config `meta` section and the `append` flag are deleted. **Consumers: run `omega migrate`.**
- [#674](../../issues/674) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `payment.products[].prices` entry must be a bare number; `{ amount: N }` is now a config error naming the product and the key. Web unwrapped the object and the backend did not, so it priced the summary right and reached the confirmation URL as `[object Object]`.
- [#602](../../issues/602) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two galleries, one shape: `/test/sections/<id>` and `/test/components/<id>`, each an index listing its own kind with frames beside every entry. One generator serves both. The `/test/sections/section/…` and `/test/sections/component/…` kind segments are gone - dev-only surface, no redirects.
- [#682](../../issues/682) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Desktop's `push-secrets` publishes through the one devkit `gh` boundary, like web and the extension: no Octokit, no libsodium, no `GH_TOKEN` PAT - `gh auth login` is the credential. A value seam base64s its file-path signing secrets, and the deploy precheck calls the shared publisher.
- [#723](../../issues/723) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Sibling shapes align: devkit's `.env` chain resolver is `envChainWatchInputs`, no longer a same-named twin of the backend's different `envWatchInputs`, and web's `collectEnvSecrets(targetDir)` takes the positional argument its desktop and extension siblings already take.
- [#727](../../issues/727) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page's last four hand-wired copy buttons - UID, signin link, push token, referral link - ride `data-omega-copy`, one feedback shape for every copy row; the share bar's label now survives a double-click instead of staying "Copied!" forever.
- [#686](../../issues/686) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Nothing in classy raises on hover any more: buttons, cards, tiles and the hero keep their highlights, rings and shadows, but every upward translate is gone - classy's own rules deleted, shared ones cancelled by an opt-out sheet other themes never load.

### Removed
- [#799](../../issues/799) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop `downloads:` mirror lane: the `mirror-downloads` task, its publish step, finalize-release's mirror half and the second repo's provisioning. One public releases repo of versionless assets already serves every download link, so the four `downloads.*` keys are retired and fail validation.

### Fixed
- [#719](../../issues/719) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The classy app shell painted an open-topped content card on a page with `topbar.enabled: false`, and a sidebar-only page had no way to open the mobile drawer. Main draws its full frame when no topbar caps it, and a floating toggle drives the existing drawer.
- [#706](../../issues/706) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every `@omega.js/web` CLI command built its logger as `new Logger('omega:<verb>')`, printing the doubled tag `[@omega.js/web:omega:build]`. Each construction now names the module alone, and a test pins the rendered build-time tag.
- [#814](../../issues/814) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A test target that names a path and matches no file fails instead of running silently green: every runner (desktop, extension, backend, web) prints `No test file matches "<target>"` and exits 1, while a run that named nothing stays green.
- [#814](../../issues/814) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - At a brand root that target is forwarded to every target, so one carrying no matching file is a no-op there (`OMEGA_TEST_FANOUT` plus a distinct exit code); the run fails, naming the target, only when no target carried it.
- [#812](../../issues/812) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The scaffolded `test/README.md` quick example runs: desktop's and the extension's showed `require('<pkg>/test/assert')`, an unexported subpath, wrapping an export shape no runner accepts. Both now show the standalone `build` form asserting through `ctx.expect`. Every tracked copy is updated; existing consumers keep theirs (defaults are copy-if-missing).
- [#810](../../issues/810) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `finalize-release` finds the release by LISTING the releases repo, drafts included, instead of a tag lookup that sees published releases only: no second draft per run, the signed Windows assets land on electron-builder's own draft, and a same-name asset is replaced rather than colliding.
- [#807](../../issues/807) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The signing runner gets a private HOME (`<runner home>\home`, a real `.gitconfig`), written at install and healed by `start`/`restart` through each runner's `.env`, so `actions/checkout` stops copying the box's symlinked `~/.gitconfig` into an unreadable junction.
- [#806](../../issues/806) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Windows desktop build writes one renderer bundle per component again: the entry key strips `index.js` behind either separator, so `about`, `main` and `settings` stop keying `index.js` alike and colliding on one output file.
- [#803](../../issues/803) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page whose renderer never fires an animation frame now clears the loading state anyway: `complete.js` drops `data-page-loading` and `aria-busy` on a 100ms fallback timer beside the frame, once, so gated forms and controls arm on a sleeping display.
- [#805](../../issues/805) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The emulator boot's orphan reap now takes firebase-tools' functions discovery server (`node_modules/.bin/firebase-functions`, or its entry file) when its hub is gone, so a killed boot no longer strands one holding a port for days.
- [#802](../../issues/802) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/devkit` asset vendoring locates a source package by walking node_modules instead of resolving its built entry, so a fresh tree's prepare no longer fails when desktop or extension prepares before web has built.
- [#799](../../issues/799) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web and extension deploy verbs dispatch to the brand repo their config names, not the git remote: inside a brand nested in another repo that answered the enclosing repo, so CI was triggered on a workflow that was never there.
- [#800](../../issues/800) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Chart tooltips name the thing being read, not the channel: a bar says "Tue: 7", a stacked segment or line "A · Tue: 7", a doughnut slice its share. `format: { prefix, suffix, decimals }` prints the unit in tooltip and value axis; `tooltip: (point) => string` overrides it.
- [#798](../../issues/798) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A dead session no longer survives in an open tab: the client re-checks it when the tab is shown, the network returns, or a request answers 401. Only a definite auth verdict signs out; network, too-many-requests and internal-error keep the user signed in.
- [#797](../../issues/797) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page's Connect button no longer re-enables while the browser is still leaving for the provider: the connect path never resolves after it sets `location.href`, so the card holds "Connecting..." until the page unloads. The back button still restores a usable form.
- [#796](../../issues/796) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `Sending response` line no longer prints a credential: at any depth, a key ending in `token`, `secret`, `password`, `privatekey` or `apikey` (case and separators ignored) logs as `***<last 4> (<n> chars)`, or `***(redacted object)` for an object. `GET /omega/user` had logged every connection token.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `runner install` / `config` walk writes EVERY answered key, the Enter-kept ones included, so one walk brings a box file written before the quoting rule up to it: each bare `KEY=value` line comes back `KEY="value"`, with no value changed. The writer stays idempotent.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `runner uninstall` keeps the `logs\` folder beside the box `.env`. `runner.log` is the trail of the uninstall itself, still held open by its tee while the home is removed. A home with neither left on disk still goes whole.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A test run can no longer touch a real signing box: every mutating `runner` subcommand refuses (naming `OMEGA_TEST_RUNNER`) unless BOTH the passed home and the module's own are scratch ones, and `sign-windows` tees nowhere. A framework suite had registered 34 orgs against the real box.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A test run cannot reach the box's machine-wide surfaces either: a mutating `runner` subcommand also refuses while the Startup folder is the real one (`OMEGA_RUNNER_STARTUP_DIR` names a scratch), and `uninstall` skips the services, logon-task and legacy-home sweeps, saying so. A case had deleted the box's Startup shortcuts.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The last two unscoped box surfaces close: a test run never kills a runner process whose path it cannot read (that path matches any home), and `deregisterOrgRunners` refuses a roster it was given no `exec`/`getRemoveToken` seam for instead of minting a real removal token.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The signing box owns its configuration: `omega runner` and `omega sign-windows` read the shell and `%LOCALAPPDATA%\omega-runner\.env` from any directory - never a project's `.env` cascade, so a brand's `GH_TOKEN` cannot configure the box. Missing required keys are asked for, else refused naming the file; every value is written double-quoted.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega runner` and `omega sign-windows` run from ANY directory: the dispatcher treats them as signing-box verbs and hands them to `@omega.js/desktop` - the host on a bare global install, else desktop from the cwd - refusing by name when it is not installed.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A scoped test target matches on Windows: `npx omega test desktop:build/runner` compared the slash-spelled scope against a backslash-spelled relative path and reported "No test files found" on every Windows box. Both sides are compared in slash form, and a backslash-spelled target from a Windows shell matches too.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows signing runner knows the electron-manager install it replaced: `runner status` names the `em-runner` home, its orgs, its live listeners and its Startup shortcuts; `uninstall` (and install's teardown) deregisters and removes them; `register-org` deletes the org-side `em-runner-<host>-<org>` first. An upgrade used to leave the old runner live.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The runner suite's "install refuses on non-Windows" case clears `GH_TOKEN` for its duration, so the token check fires where the platform check cannot. On the signing box, with the `.env` cascade's real token, it had torn down the box's runner and registered it against every admin org.
- [#789](../../issues/789) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A schema's `min` guards strings and arrays by LENGTH: a short value is refused with a 400 instead of passing, so the documented `min: 1` path-id idiom really rejects an empty id and a route stops reading the wrong document. Numbers still clamp.
- [#783](../../issues/783) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two instances can no longer spend a rotating refresh token twice: `action=refresh` takes a 30-second lease on the connection record in a Firestore transaction (the provider call times out at 20 s, inside it), the loser waits instead of calling the provider, and both answer `{ success: true, token }`.
- [#785](../../issues/785) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A PUBLIC client can use the connections lane: the default exchange and refresh omit `client_secret` entirely when `CONNECTIONS_<PROVIDER>_CLIENT_SECRET` is unset, instead of sending an empty field a token endpoint reads as a wrong secret. A confidential client's body is unchanged.
- [#782](../../issues/782) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `authorize` and `tokenize` on `/omega/user/connections` refuse a passed `uid` with a 400 naming it, instead of acting on the wrong user; `status`, `refresh` and `delete` still take one as an admin. A trusted service reads a stored token with `GET /omega/admin/firestore?path=users/<uid>`.
- [#786](../../issues/786) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A discount code covering the whole price is refused with a 400 naming the code and the price, before PayPal or Coinbase is sent the $0.00 charge neither accepts. The checkout hides both buttons, and says which code emptied the grid.
- [#771](../../issues/771) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A signed-in user can no longer forge their own connected providers or sign-in record: `connections` and `activity` join the framework-owned user-doc keys the Firestore rules refuse a client write to. A planted `refresh_token` was one `set({ merge: true })` away.
- [#737](../../issues/737) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.desktop.em.webpack.externals` now fails validation instead of passing clean: the retired bundler key sat inside the exempt `targets` namespace, so a brand still carrying it lost the setting in silence.
- [#781](../../issues/781) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Emulator processes no longer pile up forever: every boot removes the orphaned emulator-family processes earlier runs left behind, whatever project they named, and says which ones it took.
- [#778](../../issues/778) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An emulator boot no longer dies when another session's stack takes a port between the allocator's probe and firebase-tools' bind: an `EADDRINUSE` failure re-allocates around that port and boots once more, and a second failure names the port and its holder.
- [#779](../../issues/779) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Closing a live mcp-router upstream no longer kills the router two seconds later: the grace check read the transport out of the session slot that the close's own `onclose` had already emptied, so `router__disable_upstream` on a spawned child took the whole router down with it.
- [#777](../../issues/777) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The shared bundle wrapper pins esbuild's working directory per call. esbuild snapshots it at module load, so a lane that bundled first from a since-deleted temp fixture broke entry resolution for every later build - three extension self-tests, green alone, red in the full run.
- [#776](../../issues/776) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand-shape corpus's web cells build the way `omega build` does - `ensureTarget`, then the real asset lane, writing a real `dist/` the invariants read - instead of rendering against a hand-built asset-manifest stub. The font-preload invariant is a truthful check again.
- [#773](../../issues/773) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Three tier-2 theme defects: a consumer theme's `_includes/` was built as page content and broke the build, a theme shipping no `_theme.js` died on an ENOENT, and a theme shadowing a packaged id lost that skin's theme js and font files.
- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The Windows signing event log lands in the runner home the install actually uses and creates that directory on the first write, so a fresh box no longer drops its whole signing trail; `runner uninstall` deregisters each org through that org's own `config.cmd` before deleting anything.
