- [#337](../../issues/337) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `npx omega runner monitor` no longer dies with a ReferenceError on any box with a registered org: it reports each org's real state (runner directory, Startup shortcut, live listener) instead of calling two Scheduled-Task helpers that never existed.
- [#761](../../issues/761) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Skipping a PayPal free trial really skips it: a `trial.days` product carries twin plans per interval, and checkout picks the twin the buyer was granted, so a returning buyer is billed from day one and gets the discount trial products were denied. One `omega manage --service=payment` mints the no-trial twin.
- [#764](../../issues/764) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The /pricing promo banner no longer moves the page when it arrives: it is nav chrome, so it pushes the fixed nav down and nothing else. The classy opener carries the banner's mobile headroom itself (`--omega-promo-headroom`), so the masthead clears the stack from first paint.
- [#766](../../issues/766) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page or layout sheet that compiles to nothing is no longer emitted or linked. core's empty pricing, 404 and alternatives sheets each shipped as a render-blocking stylesheet request, which held those pages' first paint about a second past the home page's on a slow mobile connection.
- [#762](../../issues/762) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A head comment that mentions `<script` no longer truncates the production page: the minifier drops ordinary comments before it looks for scripts, and a page it cannot restore whole ships unminified instead of missing its charset, motion stamp and preconnects.
- [#759](../../issues/759) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A PayPal SUBSCRIPTION honors a discount code too: the discounted first period is charged at approval as the plan's setup fee and the plan's own cycles start one period later, so a subscriber pays what the confirmation page quotes and renews at the list price.
- [#758](../../issues/758) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A PayPal one-time purchase charges the discounted price: a validated discount code comes off the v2 Order the way it already comes off a Coinbase charge, so the buyer pays what the confirmation page quotes. Stripe and Chargebee were already correct.
- [#757](../../issues/757) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `omega` dispatcher runs an OMEGA package's OWN CLI inside that package's root, not one it merely devDepends on: `npx omega test` in `packages/extension` no longer hands the tree to the web framework, which scaffolded a web project into it. A CLI-less package is refused, nothing scaffolded.
- [#756](../../issues/756) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The desktop update simulator never writes the production `pendingUpdate` record: a simulated download stamps the timestamp in memory only, and a stale record from an older build (version `999.0.0`) is discarded at boot, so the next real update no longer force-installs itself.
- [#755](../../issues/755) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The link check reads a mounted (path-prefix) build: every internal link on a site served under a base path used to report as dead. Hrefs come off the mount through the same `data-omega-path-prefix` stamp the audit checks read, and a dead one is named by its site url.
- [#754](../../issues/754) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `cacheRules` entry declaring a TTL of `0` reaches Cloudflare as declared: `browserTtl: 0` (hold nothing in the browser) silently became 14400, and `edgeTtl: 0` became a year. Only an omitted TTL takes the default now, so a zone already at `0` also stops being rewritten on every run.
- [#753](../../issues/753) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A backend setup-test fix that rewrites the target `package.json` now leaves the trailing newline in place, the same bytes `ensureTarget` writes. The consumer's manifest no longer flips between two byte shapes (and no longer shows a one-line diff) depending on which writer ran last.
- [#749](../../issues/749) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The hero band carries `data-omega-first-paint`, so its copy and the headline's first rotating word paint with the document, so the LCP element is in the first paint. Authored hero slots are covered too; #763's fade is why the metric lands later.
- [#752](../../issues/752) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The dotfield canvas stops competing with first paint: its loop starts on the first idle callback after load and repaints at 30fps, not on every display frame. Reduced motion gets one still grid; the marquee measures its track a frame after the replace.
- [#482](../../issues/482) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - TikTok server conversions send the Events API 2.0 body: `event_time` in Unix seconds and one flattened `user` match block (email, phone, external id, click id, ip, user agent). The old v1.2 shape (ISO `timestamp`, nested `context`) was accepted by the endpoint and matched nobody.
- [#747](../../issues/747) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A desktop app's dev website links and its whole sign-in round trip point at the origin the local website actually serves: the baked `dev.origin`, else `OMEGA_WEBSITE_PORT` (or the baked website port) over https, else `https://localhost:4000`. `getWebsiteUrl()` and `getAuthUrl()` used to hardcode plain `http`.
- [#745](../../issues/745) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A desktop MAIN process follows a BUMPED local stack like every sibling surface: `getApiUrl()`, `getFunctionsUrl()`, `getWebsiteUrl()` and client-bridge's auth-emulator connect all read the `dev.ports` map the bundle bakes into `OMEGA_BUILD_JSON` when no `OMEGA_*_PORT` env is published, instead of assuming 5002/5001/4000/9099.
- [#742](../../issues/742) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A layout module reaches the browser boot as its namespace (`bootLayout`), so shipping no default export is legal: the redirect layout's import-time hop no longer makes every web build print an esbuild warning about an import that will always be undefined. The js lane's warning count is pinned at zero.
- [#740](../../issues/740) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page that turns the sidebar off no longer renders the topbar's drawer and rail-collapse toggles: they were dead buttons whose `aria-controls` named an id the document did not carry. Both ride the same `theme.sidebar.enabled` switch as the rail itself, on backend and admin surfaces alike.
- [#741](../../issues/741) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The shell's main region pins its own grid row, so a page that turns the topbar off keeps the page scroll inside main. It used to auto-place into the topbar's `auto` row, size to its content, and let the document scroll past the shell's ground.
- [#744](../../issues/744) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A browser extension context follows a BUMPED emulator port: `getApiUrl()` reads hosting/https from the `dev.ports` block the build bakes into `build.js`, env still winning in Node. The extension auth e2e lane boots the emulator before building, so a foreign stack on :5002 no longer fails it.
- [#739](../../issues/739) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Vendored modules resolve each other inside a published install: the prepare pass now rewrites cross-package requires inside `dist/vendor/` too and vendors any private package only a vendored file needs, so `license.js`, `target-secrets.js`, `env-watch.js` and `translate/` no longer ship bare `@omega.js/config` and `@omega.js/account` requires that throw MODULE_NOT_FOUND on first call.
- [#735](../../issues/735) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega --version` answers on a backend target: the verb's long flag was registered single-dash (`-version`), a token nobody sends, so the flag fell through to the help listing. Aliases now match every sibling CLI.
- [#734](../../issues/734) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension docs stop describing the retired fixed 16-language translate: languages come from `translation.languages` (unset = off), the source is `config/messages.json`, and the committed `translations/` cache - README, translations, build-system, the AGENTS template and the framework guide all match the code.
- [#720](../../issues/720) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend framework suite runs from a consumer install: every case file requires the framework through `dist/` and the vendored devkit, config and account under `dist/vendor/`, so `npx omega test mgr:` no longer dies on `src/` and devDependency paths the tarball never carries.
- [#708](../../issues/708) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One derivation for the persona email domain: the brand HOST (`resolvedBrandHost`), read by the seeder, test lane, reset route and dev palette alike. An apex support address no longer seeds unusable personas; a brand without `brand.url` now seeds none and the test lane refuses.
- [#712](../../issues/712) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `GET /test/roster` reads the whole seed: a project's `test/_init.js` personas, merged after the framework's own, so a consumer persona carrying a `palette` label reaches the dev palette's switcher. One without a label stays invisible.
- [#711](../../issues/711) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `site.posts` and every `site.<collection>` doc carries `content`, the document's rendered body: one flattener now serves the site arrays and `paginator.posts` alike. The newsflash homepage printed "1 min read" on its cover story and every tile while the post's own page counted the truth.
- [#716](../../issues/716) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The target checks never pull live Firestore indexes on a `cloud.shared: true` project: the sync check skips, and a missing or poisoned `firestore.indexes.json` is seeded from the template. That pull merged ~400 lines of other tenants' indexes into the consumer's authored file.
- [#714](../../issues/714) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Chargebee's `payment_failed` maps to the one-time `purchase-failed` transition. The provider routes a subscription-less failure to category one-time, but the detector read only Stripe's event string, so on Chargebee the transition never fired and the failed-payment email never sent.
- [#703](../../issues/703) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An oauth account whose accidental-signup delete FAILED is cleaned up on the next visit: the failing browser marks the uid, retries the delete at auth-ready, and signs out with the old notice when it fails again. A consent-granted account is never touched.
- [#710](../../issues/710) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The auth triggers and every `Marketing` method log identifiers, not payloads: a uid, the providers asked, a per-provider verdict. Addresses, provider results and the provider-inferred name moved behind the debug gate, and `onCreate`'s headline is now the uid alone its three siblings carry.
- [#718](../../issues/718) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The share bar's "Copy Link" confirmation swaps the real `<i>` icon to a check and back, and the runtime icon watcher redraws it. The old guard matched an `<img>` the module never builds, so only the label changed - and its dead branch read an undefined `ICON_BASE_URL`.
- [#715](../../issues/715) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The generated website build workflow parses on GitHub again: the Cloudflare purge step gates on `env.CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN`, the secret hoisted into the workflow env block. The `secrets` context is legal in no `if`, so every push scored a zero-job "workflow file issue" run.
- [#713](../../issues/713) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The self-containment gates cover every vendorable: CI's pack-smoke grep and devkit's scan derive their alternation from `VENDORABLE_PACKAGES`. Both named four while the list held six, so a raw `@omega.js/analytics` or `@omega.js/monitoring` require could ship undetected.
- [#664](../../issues/664) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An order id is minted against the intents that already exist: `payments-intents/<orderId>` is checked, a collision retries under a bounded ceiling, and an exhausted mint throws. An unchecked repeat overwrote another customer's intent and shared its analytics dedupe id.
- [#673](../../issues/673) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The one-time `purchase-refunded` and `purchase-failed` transitions send the refunded and failed-payment emails through their subscription twins' generator. Both were log-only stubs, so a refunded customer heard it from their bank statement.
- [#655](../../issues/655) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A cart with checkout activity inside its window is not abandoned: the intent route stamps `lastActivityAt` and the sweep restarts that reminder's clock from it. A shopper was mailed "Complete your checkout" 2 minutes before their purchase went through.
- [#622](../../issues/622) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand boot gates on the monorepo watch even when it did not start one, reading the running watch's log and its new vendor-propagation marker. Skipping that wait let a leg require a framework dist mid-purge, which read as "the web target never booted".
- [#617](../../issues/617) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega build` refuses while that target's `omega dev` is serving it. Both write `dist/`, so a build replaced the pages the dev server kept serving - production-stamped until a restart. The live dev run is read from its ports file; a crash leftover never blocks a build.
- [#628](../../issues/628) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `advertising.providers.adsense.enabled` and `.units` are registered retired: a brand still carrying `enabled: false` validated clean while its AdSense account started being managed. `omega migrate` drops both with the note - `client` presence is the ONE switch.
- [#650](../../issues/650) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `client.auth.config.policy`, `client.exitPopup.enabled` and `client.serviceWorker.enabled` are declared in the schema. All three work, so a brand turning one off was told it might be a typo; both switches default true and a mistyped policy is now loud.
- [#683](../../issues/683) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A target-less `requiredWhen` (`SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN`) is owed when its config path is truthy in ANY enabled target's resolved config, not the brand root alone. The monitoring service writes per-surface DSNs with the shared slot null, so the rule never fired on a canonical brand.
- [#695](../../issues/695) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The marketing field catalog has ONE per-provider view: what the walk provisions is exactly what that provider's contact sync writes. SendGrid stops warning about the two name fields it keeps in its own reserved columns, and Beehiiv stops writing two fields it never provisioned.
- [#694](../../issues/694) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand with no `brand.contact.person.name` fails the manage walk naming the key, instead of passing green and failing at the first real signup - the welcome, discount-nudge and checkup emails all sign off as that person and threw at runtime with nobody watching.
- [#726](../../issues/726) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `clipboardCopy` reports a refused clipboard: both lanes reject - the legacy lane reads `execCommand`'s false return - so the copy controls' "Failed to copy" is reachable and a denial is never announced as a success.
- [#728](../../issues/728) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `CLOUDFLARE_TOKEN` delivers `web: 'ci'`: the publish lane pushes the repo Actions secret, so the build workflow's Cloudflare purge step can finally fire, and the delivery renderer treats the key as workflow-owned - never a duplicate of the template's own mapping.
- [#729](../../issues/729) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Chargebee one-time purchases fire `purchase-completed`: the provider subscribes `payment_succeeded` and routes the subscription-less one to category one-time, so a paid sale sends its receipt and analytics. A renewal's charge stays uncategorized - `subscription_renewed` owns that revenue.
- [#730](../../issues/730) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The stale pid-record reap proves a live pid's PARENT belongs to the dead run before signalling - a recycled number on another brand's live jar is left alone - waits out the reaped record's ports, and the boot's reap-before-record order is pinned by test.
- [#731](../../issues/731) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `llms.txt` honors `meta.index` on posts: a post the brand took out of search, the sitemap and pages.json is no longer handed to LLMs.
- [#732](../../issues/732) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Retired PATHS match inside a target's instance array: a legacy key on a multi-instance target bounces like any other, the error naming the real indexed path - and `omega migrate` deletes it there too.
- [#733](../../issues/733) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A broken project `test/_init.js` no longer sticks for the process lifetime: the seed-account loader caches only successful reads and drops the failed module from require's cache, so a fixed file is picked up on the next request.
- [#707](../../issues/707) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A target scaffolded before the `setup` retirement heals on the walk: a script still chaining the retired `npx omega setup` sheds that leg, the dead `setup` script goes, and `omega dev`'s legs stop dying at `Unknown command "setup"`.
- [#697](../../issues/697) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A trial conversion books as `trial_convert` whatever order the webhooks land in: the label reads a claimed trial with no recorded outcome, not the doc's in-trial-ness at charge time. The converting charge stamps `trial.outcome`, so month two is still a renewal.
- [#649](../../issues/649) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The seven SendGrid unsubscribe-group ids leave the code: the campaigns service provisions each group by name and writes its id to `marketing.campaigns.providers.sendgrid.groups.<key>`, where the send path reads it. A missing id fails the send loudly. **Legacy brands: run `npx omega manage` once.**
- [#585](../../issues/585) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Above-the-fold text no longer waits for the client bundle: the reveal starter moved out of the main bundle (behind firebase/auth init) into `core/js/first-paint.js`, its own tiny module loaded ahead of it and run at DOMContentLoaded. Same engine, same transition - just earlier.
- [#593](../../issues/593) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand's declared collection publishes as `site.<name>` in the built-ins' Jekyll doc shape, so a migrated `{% for product in site.products %}` renders its documents instead of looping over nothing on a green build. A name a build fact or a config section already owns is rejected loudly.
- [#596](../../issues/596) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A collection document may name itself `<namespace>.name` beside `<namespace>.title` (`title` wins when both exist). Documents spelling `product.name` derived their title, and every `<title>` and h1, from a title-cased slug.
- [#597](../../issues/597) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The slugifier DELETES apostrophes instead of separating on them, straight and typographic: `Beginner's Guides` slugs to `beginners-guides` the way UJM published it, so a migrating brand's category URLs stay put.
- [#598](../../issues/598) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The blog hub's featured card counts the featured post's real body: `paginator.posts` documents carry Jekyll's `post.content` (lazily, at render time), so the card no longer reads "1 min read" for a twelve-minute post.
- [#589](../../issues/589) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A legacy Jekyll `collections:` block is dropped with its manual step named per collection, mapping and array spellings alike: no Jekyll collection carries the grouping `field` `targets.web.collections` requires, so carrying it verbatim wrote a config whose FIRST build died.
- [#594](../../issues/594) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `authorizedFetch()` → `omega.request()` rewrite carries the two shapes the rename left dead: `response: 'json'` is dropped (the client returns the parsed body) and `err.status` becomes `err.code` inside the catch block that binds it. Everything else is named line by line.
- [#595](../../issues/595) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A migrated page reading `random_id` gets the `{% assign %}` UJM handed every page for free, so the read renders an id instead of the empty string it became.
- [#671](../../issues/671) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A page's own `meta:` block no longer reads the walk it feeds (block and flow spellings; every key the drop empties goes too, because a childless key is a null that REPLACES the default), and the config file's own `{{ site.<section> }}` values move with it, `--check` included.
- [#601](../../issues/601) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The built-output link check parses whole attributes and honors the quote that opened a value, so an `href=` written INSIDE a single-quoted attribute stops reporting a bare-backslash dead link on every page carrying it.
- [#657](../../issues/657) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `beforeCreate`, `beforeSignIn` and `onDelete` auth triggers open with the uid alone. Each used to write the whole user record (email, display name, password hash) and its trigger context into Cloud Logging on every fire; record and context now sit at debug.
- [#630](../../issues/630) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - All 430 case files export through `defineCases()`, so `node --test` on one throws naming `npx omega test` instead of reporting `pass 1` for a file whose cases never ran. A devkit guard fails any unwrapped case file.
- [#537](../../issues/537) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A payment lookup failure names the call that missed: a refund or credit-note miss thrown from `getRefundDetails()` said `fetchResource(...)`, pointing whoever read the log at a function that never ran.
- [#706](../../issues/706) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A refused command reads the same on every framework: the crafted refusal prints its lines alone, no raw stack under it, no ✗ in front. An unparseable package.json up the walk refuses by name too, and the dispatcher's message lists the `create`/`new` spellings it already allowed.
- [#719](../../issues/719) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The account page's fifteen copy buttons are named for a screen reader, the extension mockup is marked decorative rather than read out icon by icon, and the site alerts dismiss through a real button with one live-region semantic.
- [#629](../../issues/629) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One stop-signal list (`@omega.js/devkit/stop-signals`: SIGINT, SIGTERM, SIGHUP) serves every supervisor: the emulator, `omega test`, `omega dev` and the log tails. A closed terminal now runs the full teardown instead of orphaning the firebase tree onto PID 1.
- [#722](../../issues/722) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A test run that starts its OWN emulator takes the whole stack down - emulator AND test-runner child - on SIGTERM and SIGHUP too, not Ctrl+C alone. A stop from anything but a terminal killed the CLI where it stood and left what it had spawned running.
- [#721](../../issues/721) [`c7a777ab`](../../commit/c7a777ab) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A boot reaps the previous run's leftovers BY PID from that run's record, before overwriting it - the port sweep never saw the reload watcher, which binds nothing. It signals only a recorded pid still reading as this project's emulator machinery, and only when the record's root is gone.

## [0.49.0] 2026-08-30

### Added
- [#698](../../issues/698) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Namecheap call refused for a non-whitelisted IP is walked, not warned: the domain service names the caller IP, gates on Enter to open the API access page, and rechecks the refused call until it passes. Non-interactive runs warn and continue.
- [#661](../../issues/661) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `/signin?authPrivateKey=<api.privateKey>` signs in from a URL that keeps working: the auth pages trade the key for a fresh custom token at `POST /omega/user/token` on every load, then strip it from the address bar. Custom tokens expire in an hour; a saved dock URL does not.
- [#693](../../issues/693) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The campaigns service creates SendGrid link branding for `emailurl.<domain>` when the account has none, writes its two CNAMEs into Cloudflare, waits for SendGrid validation, and flips the record to proxied in the same walk, so emailed links land on HTTPS with no console visit.
- [#687](../../issues/687) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `brands/naked-brand`: a bare fixture brand (brand.id, brand.name, one website target, demo cloud, nothing else) so a QA walkthrough sees every setup prompt fire from zero. Offline and test-only forever; nothing provisions real cloud resources from it.
- [#608](../../issues/608) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One setup contract for every manage service: a missing credential is asked for right then, with Provide / Skip this run / Disable permanently (`<service>.enabled: false`). Non-interactive runs never prompt - they print the exact keys and step aside.

### Changed
- [#636](../../issues/636) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Write ops reconcile fully: the assets cache and the brand root's composed CI workflows delete every framework-written file the current config no longer derives, so dropping a target removes its workflow files on the next walk. Human-authored files are never touched; cert delivery stays warn-only.

### Removed
- [#636](../../issues/636) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The checkout's crypto pay button, its `providers.coinbase` reads and the `crypto → coinbase` provider mapping, with the config stub they depended on.
- [#636](../../issues/636) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `COINBASE_API_KEY`, its `payment.providers.coinbase` config stub and `APOLLO_API_KEY`: keys with no reader anywhere - there is no coinbase payment provider (the lane is stripe, paypal, chargebee, test) and no Apollo enrichment. A brand config still carrying the stub now says so in a validator warning.

### Fixed
- [#661](../../issues/661) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A key in the address bar never reaches Sentry: the failed private-key sign-in strips it before it captures, and the browser SDK scrubs `authPrivateKey` and `authCustomToken` out of navigation breadcrumbs and event request urls.
- [#699](../../issues/699) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A verb run outside any target refuses instead of scaffolding one there: the dispatcher's no-context fallback allows only onboard, help, version, cwd and logs, and every framework's ensure-target refuses a workspace root by name. `npx omega onboard` in a fresh clone still works.
- [#702](../../issues/702) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The user-delete route's internal sessions call drops `log: true`, so wonderful-fetch stops printing its configuration - the admin-key header included - into Cloud Logging. A source-scan test fails any options object pairing that flag with a credential header.
- [#701](../../issues/701) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A rate-limited OAuth signup says so: the returning redirect result resolves inside the return leg's own try, so a blocking-function rejection (`auth/error-code:-47`) shows its message and re-enables the form instead of escaping uncaught and leaving disabled fields and spinners.
- [#700](../../issues/700) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Signup stops waiting on the server: the web auth listener fires the `/omega/user/signup` POST without awaiting it, the legacy consent guard is deleted, and an account doc that is not written yet is the normal state - only a rules permission-denied signs the user out.
- [#692](../../issues/692) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The edge service reads SendGrid's domain-auth values live off the SendGrid API when building DNS records, so those records exist on any authenticated brand; unauthenticated brands get a printed skip. **Legacy brands: delete `edge.providers.cloudflare.dns.sendgrid` from omega.json5 - ignored now.**
- [#685](../../issues/685) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The client's `request.js` and package main drop their guarded `module.exports` tail: bundled to CJS, the tail ran last and clobbered the bundle's own exports. Pure ESM is the one mechanism now, and the web test harness imports the real module instead of a stub.
- [#667](../../issues/667) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An Internal OAuth consent screen STOPS the manage walk: Google offers no API write for the audience, so the run opens the console page on ENTER and polls until the flip lands. Skip warns; `cloud.consentAudience: false` records "never ask again". An org-less create failure names its cause.
- [#656](../../issues/656) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - GA4 deduplicates `purchase` on `transaction_id`, so a money event now carries ONE CHARGE's id instead of the subscription's: the order id on a first purchase or trial start, the provider's charge id on a renewal, recovery or conversion, the reversed charge's id on a refund. Renewals stop being dropped.
- [#646](../../issues/646) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Links in transactional email land on HTTPS: `emailurl.<domain>` is created PROXIED once SendGrid reports the branded link `valid: true` (`GET /v3/whitelabel/links`), so Cloudflare terminates TLS instead of SendGrid serving no certificate. Until then the DNS ensure warns, naming the record. Legacy brands need the one-time flip.
- [#641](../../issues/641) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The OAuth2 providers log the provider, the uid and whether the token exchange succeeded, nothing else. All three used to write the exchange response (access, refresh and id tokens) into Cloud Logging in plain text, and the identity fetches printed their authorization headers. `auth:on-create` logs the uid alone.
- [#636](../../issues/636) [`3740777e`](../../commit/3740777e) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The schema declares what the services actually read and write back (`certificates`, both `domain` roles, the TikTok `appId`, `advertising.fallback`/`tags` and more), and the validator WARNS naming every undeclared key.

## [0.48.0] 2026-08-30

### Added
- [#645](../../issues/645) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One doc per manager service in `docs/manager/`: what each of the 28 reconciles, its config keys, its gotchas, with the map linking them. The edge doc carries the subdomain gotcha: a two-label api host stays DNS-only, so Cloudflare geo headers never arrive. A test holds the parity.
- [#626](../../issues/626) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `requiredWhen` on env schema entries: a key must be non-empty when its config path is set. One checker feeds the backend boot (production refuses), the desktop and extension bakes (build mode refuses), and the manage walk (warns per enabled target). Replaces the scattered hand checks.
- [#448](../../issues/448) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - TikTok setup mints its own token: paste the app secret once (never saved), walk the portal authorization, exchange inline, and only `TIKTOK_ACCESS_TOKEN` reaches the brand `.env`.

### Changed
- [#644](../../issues/644) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend wakeup ping fires from every entry point whose first user action is a backend call: eight web pages, the newsletter band, download, the desktop auth bridge and the extension surfaces. One shared `WAKEUP_ROUTE` (`/omega/health`) from `@omega.js/client` replaces web's `PAYMENT_WARMUP_ROUTE`.
- [#662](../../issues/662) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A manage service WAITS for what it needs instead of owing a rerun: SendGrid's branded-link validation before proxying `emailurl`, a new zone's nameservers before the registrar write, GA's release of a just-deleted Firebase link. Each wait skips with `s`, warning with its reason.
- [#627](../../issues/627) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - How an env key reaches each target is one schema declaration (`delivery`: env, ci, or bake): one renderer derives every workflow secrets block, bake list, and published secret set. A secret only bakes when declared public at rest; desktop's workflow now composes to the brand root.
- [#678](../../issues/678) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The brand-root `.env` is the one file humans and the manager edit; a target `.env` is an optional per-key override no machine writes. Every verb composes the target's runtime env from the schema-filtered file cascade, `deliverAs` renaming on delivery; the CI secret publishers read the same composed env.
- [#675](../../issues/675) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` is retired on every framework: its local half runs as `ensureTarget()` at the start of every verb, its network half as a deploy precheck with `--no-secrets` to skip it. A bare `omega` prints help; backend's setup checks ride `omega test`; extension migration is its own `omega migrate`.

### Fixed
- [#691](../../issues/691) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The mcp-router oneshot deadline test stops flaking under lane load: its elapsed-time assertion gets 10ms of slack for Node firing timers a hair before Date.now agrees.
- [#690](../../issues/690) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega dev`'s programmatic shutdown no longer leaks the firebase emulator: each leg spawns in its own process group and shutdown signals the group, so the grandchild dies with npm instead of orphaning onto the classic ports.
- [#688](../../issues/688) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The dev-watch suite stops flaking on a lost first edit: a save landing before the fresh watch root's FSEvents stream initializes is re-fired until the watcher wakes, and a woken-but-stale rebuild still fails.
- [#675](../../issues/675) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A freshly onboarded brand boots: the workspace walk fills each target's missing npm scripts from its framework's `projectScripts` declaration, so `omega dev`'s fan-out finds its legs before any per-target verb has run.
- [#666](../../issues/666) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A subscription that sells no trial stops asking `/omega/payments/trial-eligibility` on checkout: the question only goes out when the product's `trial.days` is above 0, so a trial-less plan prices itself out of build config with no authed round trip and no skeleton wait, exactly like a one-time buy.
- [#643](../../issues/643) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The manage run summary names what warned: each service's breakdown lists its warned operations with the reason each gave, instead of one "some operations had issues" line per service.
- [#680](../../issues/680) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension deploy precheck publishes its store credentials and GA secret as repo Actions secrets, like web and desktop, so a first `publish.yml` run stops failing on secrets nobody set. `--no-secrets` opts out.
- [#679](../../issues/679) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The live-page feed poller pauses in a covered tab and resumes with one immediate read on return, so a background dashboard stops burning its host's rate limit. A visible-but-unfocused window keeps polling.
- [#668](../../issues/668) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One-time purchases stop being priced and labelled as subscriptions: `once` from the pricing click through checkout, the intent and the receipt; `begin_checkout` carries the real price instead of $0; the receipt names the product; and the confirmation page opens confirmed instead of polling for a plan.
- [#665](../../issues/665) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A detected transition is claimed on `payments-orders/{orderId}.transitions.<name>` in a transaction: the loser dispatches no handler, fires no analytics and runs no marketing sync, so a concurrent checkout pair sends one order email, not two. The claim speaks for ten minutes, and a failed run's retry retakes its own.
- [#652](../../issues/652) [`43a5a661`](../../commit/43a5a661) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every catalog mapping re-checked against the live GA4, Meta and TikTok specs, their links pinned in the catalog header: TikTok's retired `CompletePayment` becomes `Purchase`, `trial_start` becomes `StartTrial`, every real charge is a `Purchase` instead of a `Subscribe`, and eight misused standard events become honest custom events.

## [0.47.0] 2026-08-28

### Added
- [#637](../../issues/637) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The page paint contract (`docs/web/page-contract.md`): static content paints at once, user data arrives through bindings, every wait has a deadline and a named fallback, and `FormManager` gates hold a submit until its answers land. Checkout paints prices before eligibility answers; `omega.request(url, { wakeup: true })` warms the backend.
- [#577](../../issues/577) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every match parameter Meta, TikTok, and GA4 accept is plumbed, one normalization table per provider; the fire log names which match keys went (key NAMES only); the dev palette gains an **Ad match keys** readout.

### Changed
- [#637](../../issues/637) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A trial-eligibility check that times out or fails is UNKNOWN, not "not eligible": checkout quotes no trial and charges the full amount today, while the intent payload still asks for the trial so the server grants it to a buyer who qualifies.
- [#639](../../issues/639) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - ONE key per AI provider: `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, the `OMEGA_*` twins removed outright, a company-wide key served by the company `.env` layer, and a new `ai` manage service that asks for both once (`ai.enabled: false` to stop asking).
- [#577](../../issues/577) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The server half of `sign_up` fires from the post-auth request (`routes/user/signup`), not the auth trigger, so Meta and TikTok get IP, user agent, and platform cookies (`attribution.cookies`); same `sign_up.<uid>` dedupe id, one fire per account.

### Removed
- [#634](../../issues/634) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The provider webhook signing secrets are gone: `?key=` against `OMEGA_WEBHOOK_KEY` is the one check every payment webhook and dispute alert rides, so the Stripe and Chargeblast signature layers, their `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` / `CHARGEBLAST_WEBHOOK_SECRET` env keys (and the `_DEV` twin), and the key-only warning are removed outright.

### Fixed
- [#670](../../issues/670) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega dev` on a linked brand waits for the monorepo watch's initial prepare before booting any target, so a leg no longer loads a `dist/` the watch is still rewriting. Bounded at 120s, with one warning naming the packages still preparing.
- [#654](../../issues/654) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A trial checkout's confirmation page fires `trial_start` with value 0 instead of a `purchase`. The server fired `trial_start` for the same order, and two event names never deduplicate, so Meta and TikTok counted the browser half as a second conversion. Both halves now key on `trial_start.<order id>`.
- [#635](../../issues/635) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every value a brand must supply is ASKED for at the moment of need instead of warned about and skipped: the TikTok app id and redirect URI, an ambiguous Sentry organization, the Apple bundle id prefix, and the CAN-SPAM mailing address. `OMEGA_WEBHOOK_KEY` is minted in place, never asked for.
- [#569](../../issues/569) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `UNSUBSCRIBE_HMAC_KEY` is minted like every other brand-generated key: the onboard stub and a new manage-time `env-keys` step render from one list, so a brand missing it gains one and disperse composes it into the backend that same run. A backend booting without it names the key; production refuses to boot.
- [#570](../../issues/570) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A manage run leaves EXACTLY ONE payment webhook endpoint on the brand's API host: every other `/omega/payments/webhook` endpoint there (the legacy `?processor=` twin, which took every event and answered 400) is deleted with one loud redacted-URL line - Stripe, PayPal and Chargebee alike. Other hosts are never touched.
- [#631](../../issues/631) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The AdSense sites check reads a subdomain brand's state from the apex entry that covers it, instead of warning the site is missing: AdSense lists sites at the apex only, and the reported line names the parent it is covered by.
- [#638](../../issues/638) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Signup fills `personal.location.country/region/city` from the request's geolocation when they are empty, so the ad-platform match keys (`ct`/`st`/`country`) and the email merge fields stop shipping blank - a location the user set is never overwritten.
- [#633](../../issues/633) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The post-auth signup request fires once per account: a per-uid in-flight marker (`temporary.signupMetadata.<uid>`) gates the post while the server writes `flags.signupProcessed`. A failed send clears the marker on the spot, and a marker older than 10 minutes counts as absent.
- [#632](../../issues/632) [`916955d6`](../../commit/916955d6) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A log line names a user by address or uid and a payload by its key names, never by serializing the document: `Email.send()`, the signup lines, the `Usage` lines and `test/authenticate` used to write `api.privateKey`, consent, the request IP and attribution into Cloud Logging in plain text.

## [0.46.0] 2026-08-27

### Added
- [#584](../../issues/584) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.backend.projectType: 'custom'` runs the backend as its own server on `PORT` for a container host (Render & co): same routes, same auth middleware, no Cloud Functions. Deploy and test go through the target's own scripts; the Firebase-only verbs refuse and name their lane.
- [#612](../../issues/612) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` at a brand root deletes every retired key from `config/omega.json5` in place: the key, its subtree and the comment documenting it, one line each naming the replacement. Idempotent, `--dry-run` for the plan, every other byte preserved.
- [#522](../../issues/522) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate:rules` (alias `migrate:firestore-rules`): the run-alone backend verb that moves a legacy `firestore.rules` onto the compiled model and repoints `firebase.json`, loud about the live-posture change it makes.
- [#587](../../issues/587) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A brand-root `omega dev` starts the monorepo watch as a session child whenever the brand's `@omega.js/*` deps resolve into a monorepo checkout: one terminal now covers framework edits too. Lock-aware, dies with the stack; a registry-installed brand says so in one line.
- [#529](../../issues/529) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The translate engine dedupes a page's cold strings before batching: each unique string is translated once and fanned back to every occurrence, so a title sent by three meta tags stops costing three translations. The #523 split-and-re-ask stays the backstop.
- [#567](../../issues/567) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` scaffolds the backend's socket-free STATIC test lane: the connect trap (any TCP connect or DNS lookup throws), three skeleton suites under `test/_unit/`, and a `test` script that preloads the trap. `npm test` is that lane now; the emulator lane is `npm run test:emulator`.
- [#441](../../issues/441) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The hero takes a consumer-supplied animated visual: one folder at `src/_hero/<name>/` (`index.html`, `style.scss`, `script.js`, bundled like section assets), named by `demo: { type: custom, name }`. The framework ships `orbit` as the reference, reduced-motion branch included.
- [#513](../../issues/513) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The marketing/hero section takes an optional `breadcrumb` trail: an ordered `{ label, href }` list rendered as a labeled nav above the headline, the last crumb the current page and unlinked; absent renders nothing.
- [#515](../../issues/515) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - New `marketing/prose` section: the standard head cluster over a `body` of paragraphs, for the head-plus-lede band that previously had to fake itself with an item-less showcase.
- [#493](../../issues/493) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - New `marketing/pricing-cards` section, composed by the base index layout under the WHY band: one compact card per plan, read from the same resolved catalog `/pricing` renders, so no homepage number is ever a copy.
- [#514](../../issues/514) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A marketing/bento tile takes an optional `href` and becomes ONE anchor wearing the shared interactive states, so a hub or category listing composes the band instead of hand-rolling a grid of linked cards; a linked tile's body carries no nested anchors.
- [#519](../../issues/519) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The marketing/bento band takes `numbered: true`, auto-numbering its tiles 1..N by position as a small ordinal chip, so a process band keeps its steps and reordering renumbers itself.
- [#518](../../issues/518) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - marketing/stats items take an optional `icon` and a `color` naming a slot in the categorical token palette (never a raw hex), so a stat rail keeps the glyph and accent its legacy carried.
- [#550](../../issues/550) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A REFUSED payment webhook reports itself to Sentry as ONE warning carrying the refusal reason, the provider and the event ids, so a forged owner or a resource the provider never had surfaces on the dashboard, not just in the logs. A processed event, or an unset DSN, reports nothing.
- [#581](../../issues/581) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `@omega.js/config` gains the env schema: one inventory of every env key OMEGA needs - owner, targets, minted or third-party, secret, required, description. The manager's mint, `.env` grouping and disperse composition, plus the backend's reader, all derive from it: a new key is one entry.
- [#462](../../issues/462) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `socials` is empty by default and the footer loops only the entries a brand defines, replacing the hardcoded five-platform fallback that drew icons for accounts a brand never had.
- [#463](../../issues/463) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The section library at `/test/sections`: one page per section, every variation in its own autosizing frame with its authored args beside it, referenced from the sections docs.
- [#470](../../issues/470) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` names the retired `asset_path` frontmatter key and its replacement instead of reporting it as an ignored content key indistinguishable from copy.
- [#472](../../issues/472) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The about layout gains a standalone stats band with its own head cluster, so a legacy "By the numbers" heading has a home instead of vanishing.
- [#475](../../issues/475) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `hero.demo.placement: side` renders the demo beside the copy; the default stays centred under the CTAs.
- [#476](../../issues/476) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Hero buttons render their authored `icon` and `class` again (the legacy `rocket` + `btn-light` shapes); unauthored buttons stay byte-identical.
- [#478](../../issues/478) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Self-healing config: every manage run materializes schema-defined defaults into `omega.json5`, so a consumer's config stays complete as the schema grows.
- [#479](../../issues/479) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One machine-record file, `.omega/state.json`, sectioned per fact kind: deploy records move back in from the `.omega/deploys.json` that 0.45.0 introduced.
- [#480](../../issues/480) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A bridged desktop renderer's `setUserId` throws, naming main's `manager.analytics.setUserId(uid)` as the call to make, instead of silently no-oping.
- [#486](../../issues/486) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `hero.demo.type: video` renders a video demo in the side or centred column.
- [#496](../../issues/496) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A side-placed hero demo and the centred visual are either/or: side wins and the visual moves below the fold.

### Changed
- [#606](../../issues/606) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The page chrome no longer defines empty `gtag`/`fbq`/`ttq` for an unconfigured provider. The guarded browser transport already reads a missing global as a silent skip, so the stubs only made a pixel that never loaded look present.
- [#610](../../issues/610) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `targets.web.download` and `targets.web.extension` are retired, and `site.download`/`site.extension` are gone. The `/download` page, the `/extension` page and their shortlinks read the curated `site.targets` view alone. A config still carrying either key fails validation naming the block it derives from.
- [#604](../../issues/604) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Translation batches run `CONCURRENCY` wide (5) instead of one at a time, results stitched back in batch order. The sentinel, alignment checks and the #523 split backstop stay per batch, unchanged.
- [#605](../../issues/605) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The framework excludes its OWN default pages from translation - auth, the app shell, account, payment, portal, legal, redirect stubs - derived from the defaults tree. `translation.exclude` is for brand pages only now.
- [#581](../../issues/581) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend reads every brand-supplied env key through one reader (`libraries/env.js`), and `Manager.init()` refuses to boot in EVERY environment - development included - naming every missing required key. **A brand whose `.env` lacks a minted key must run `npx omega manage` before its backend boots.**
- [#527](../../issues/527) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - AdSense has ONE switch: `advertising.providers.adsense.client` presence drives the managed account, the rendered units and the `ads.txt` record together. `advertising.providers.adsense.enabled` is deleted - it no longer skips the manager's service, and the interactive Disable answer opts the provider out (`advertising.providers.adsense: false`) instead. The gating doctrine behind it is in `docs/shared/config.md`.
- [#539](../../issues/539) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The homepage plan band renders `/pricing`'s feature lists through the same new `pricing/features` component, so a feature the catalog explains carries its hover definition on the homepage too. The line introducing a tier's extras reads "Everything in <previous>, and:" on both surfaces.
- [#540](../../issues/540) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The section gallery is a rail plus a content pane that stacks EVERY variant, one frame each with its options, over the args table. The rail's variant links are plain anchor jumps, the gallery head clears the site masthead, and the "← Section library" back link is reachable again.
- [#549](../../issues/549) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `/test/components` is the component gallery: every resolved `_components` entry on one page, wearing the section gallery's rail-plus-stacked-frames shell. The living styleguide that held that URL moved to `/test/styleguide`, whole, with the same dev-page rules.
- [#490](../../issues/490) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A website target's link exceptions live in `config/link-exceptions.json5`: json5 like every other consumer surface, so the reason an exception exists travels beside it in a comment instead of some worksheet. A file left at the retired `.json` name fails loudly with the rename.
- [#538](../../issues/538) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every `omega_icon` wrapper ships `aria-hidden="true"`, so decorative icons leave the accessibility tree by declaration instead of by the convention that screen readers skip text-free SVGs; the rare meaningful icon passes `label="…"` and renders `role="img"` + `aria-label` instead. One mechanism change, no call-site edits.
- [#555](../../issues/555) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A gallery frame carries no overlay chrome: the frame page bakes the consent gate and the chat widget off beside the nav and footer it already drops, and `main.js` skips the dev palette inside an iframe. A normal page still ships all three.
- [#554](../../issues/554) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A production build emits NO page at `/test` or under `/test/` (framework dev surfaces and consumer overrides alike), the omission the `/test/sections` gallery already rode. Dev builds keep them all; the boundary is a path segment, so `/testimonials` ships.
- [#547](../../issues/547) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Markdown renders with `typographer: true` on both markdown-it instances, so a brand converted off Jekyll keeps Kramdown's smart quotes, apostrophes, dashes and ellipses instead of drifting into straight punctuation on its next build. No consumer knob. **This changes rendered punctuation on every brand at its next build.**
- [#573](../../issues/573) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A scaffolded extension's `config/messages.json` seeds `appDescription` from `brand.description` when it fits the 200-character store cap, so the one field every consumer hand-edited arrives written. A longer or absent description keeps the generic phrasing.
- [#576](../../issues/576) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A compiled extension manifest carries `homepage_url`, baked from `brand.url` on every browser target, so the Chrome and Firefox store listings link the brand's site. A declared value wins; no `brand.url` ships no key.
- [#583](../../issues/583) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An extension's default `externally_connectable.matches` is the brand's own origin from `brand.url`, with the localhost dev origin in dev builds only, so a brand site can message its published extension. A declared consumer value still wins; a scaffolded boot test asserts the brand origin.
- [#481](../../issues/481) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - docs/shared/breaking-changes.md names the backend test-discovery change: only `*.test.js` files run, so a plain `.js` legacy test needs renaming or it is silently invisible.
- [#483](../../issues/483) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - docs/shared/config.md maps legacy `socials` to the root key the schema defines, not `targets.web`.
- [#484](../../issues/484) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `testing` config key, read by nothing, leaves the canonical order; the legacy `testing.apiCheck` and `testing.endpoints` keys are named as retired.

### Fixed
- [#615](../../issues/615) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The web watch suites derive their rebuild deadline from the machine's measured contention (30s floor × load per CPU, capped) instead of a flat 30s, and every timeout names the elapsed time, the derivation and the last rebuild event seen.
- [#614](../../issues/614) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` on a `projectType: 'custom'` backend scaffolds no Firebase-only artifact - no `firebase.json`, rules, or index files - and drops the checks that maintain them. An authored one is kept, untouched.
- [#613](../../issues/613) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `site.time` is set once per build, so a post's `article:modified_time` and its JSON-LD `dateModified` carry a real date instead of the empty string every brand shipped. Both name the same instant as `site.omega.date.iso`.
- [#611](../../issues/611) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate`'s `config-reads` rule rewrites every `site.<section>` read (page bodies, frontmatter, section descriptors) to `resolved.config.<section>`, and the build now fails on a missed one naming file, line and expression. Only Liquid context counts: a hostname, a filename, a fenced code sample and a `{% raw %}` body are not reads.
- [#579](../../issues/579) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` writes `enabled: true` onto a `hero.secondary_button` block that has no `enabled` key, and the report warns that it did. `marketing/hero` defaults the button off and a UJM page opted in by authoring the block, so migrated pages built clean with the CTA gone. The section default is unchanged.
- [#565](../../issues/565) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` converts a class-only Kramdown attribute list on a link - `[text](url){: .a .b }` → `<a href="url" class="a b">text</a>` - in markdown files only, and reports every other surviving `{: … }` as an error. The engine still loads no attrs plugin.
- [#559](../../issues/559) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` converts the retired adunit includes: `{% include modules/adunits/adsense.html type=… vert-size=… %}` becomes `{% section "verts/unit", type: …, size: … %}`. An arg the section has no home for is a warning, and any other `adunits/*` include an error - an unresolved include renders nothing and fails nothing.
- [#560](../../issues/560) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` rewrites the UJM library accessor: `webManager.uj().X()` / `omega.uj().X()` become `omega.library().X()`. A `.uj(` the rule cannot claim - an unknown receiver, a call with args - is an error finding naming the successor, not a build-clean file whose first call throws.
- [#548](../../issues/548) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A web build caches the six Firebase auth helper files per project under the brand's `.omega`, and a failed fetch now serves that copy with a dated warn instead of failing a build that already emitted every page. A failure with nothing cached stays fatal; `demo-*` projects still skip.
- [#546](../../issues/546) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The three service switches the manager reads are declared in the schema at last: `repo.providers.github.enabled`, `search.providers.searchConsole.enabled` and `edge.providers.cloudflare.enabled`, boolean, default on. A misspelled one now fails validation instead of silently reading as on.
- [#509](../../issues/509) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A payment webhook's write is steered by the uid on the PROVIDER's record; a payload claiming a different one refuses the event outright, stamped `uid-mismatch` and loud, so an event naming a real subscription with a forged uid stops moving it onto that uid.
- [#510](../../issues/510) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A refund's amount, currency and reason come from provider data - Stripe's charge, PayPal's own refund record, Chargebee's credit note - so a payload claiming an inflated refund stops booking that number onto the order, the refund email and the conversion.
- [#511](../../issues/511) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend mcp and verts suites address the lane they booted (`config.apiUrl`) instead of a hardcoded `localhost:5002`, so a port-bumped run stops firing its 24 tests at whatever holds the classic port.
- [#495](../../issues/495) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend cron-job recipe documents the shape the runner actually calls (an async function taking `{ Manager, ctx, context, libraries }`), so a job written to the doc stops silently never running; consumer paths in `routes.md`/`file-naming.md`/`test-framework.md` are src-first, and the consumer tree names `payment-providers/`.
- [#522](../../issues/522) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` no longer migrates a legacy `firestore.rules` in place: while `firebase.json` still points at the brand's own file, both rules checks report the deferral with the migration pointer and leave every byte alone, so a deploy chain can never flip live rules posture unasked.
- [#500](../../issues/500) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The marketing/hero headline no longer hardcodes `h1`: a `heading_level` arg (default 1) lets a hero-shaped band below the fold author 2, so a page composing the section twice ships one h1 instead of two.
- [#491](../../issues/491) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `frontend/core/minimal` layout gates its masthead h1 on the breadcrumb being present, so a document page with none stops shipping a blank first h1 above its body.
- [#512](../../issues/512) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A marketing/bento band with no `subheadline` renders no sub line at all instead of inheriting the section's demo sentence; the demo copy moved to the gallery variant, and the same sweep emptied the about timeline and principles sub lines.
- [#520](../../issues/520) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The about/letter aside gates on having something to say: empty `feed_items` removes the feed frame, status chip and label, and with no aside photo either the aside column goes too and the letter runs full width - no more empty ornament on a brand that only wanted the mission copy.
- [#516](../../issues/516) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The about hero's facts head passes `headline_accent` through like every other head composition, so an accented stats headline stops rendering without its accent word.
- [#517](../../issues/517) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The team hub gives its brand copy somewhere to land: an optional head cluster over the portrait grid, an optional one-line `bio` per member under name and position, and an optional headline over the mission band; all absent renders the hub exactly as before.
- [#499](../../issues/499) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Autoplaying video parks under `prefers-reduced-motion`: the shared motion engine strips autoplay, pauses the player and shows its controls for every `video[autoplay]` on the page, so the hero video demo and marketing/product-demo stop moving for a visitor who asked for stillness.
- [#521](../../issues/521) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The internal link check reads `<pre>` blocks as code DISPLAY, not markup: an escaped `href=` inside one (a docs snippet, the section gallery's copyable args) is characters on the page, so a demo variant can author a real link again - a real anchor at the same URL still fails.
- [#488](../../issues/488) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - One slugifier answers a taxonomy term on both sides: the term is LINKED at the slug its page is GENERATED at, so `A&R` stops shipping `/blog/tags/a-and-r` against a page written at `/blog/tags/a-r`. Eleventy's own `slugify` no longer shadows the framework's in templates.
- [#489](../../issues/489) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate --check` knows the tags the lane converts INTO: `{% section %}`, `{% component %}` and `{% composition %}` are registered in the lint pass, so a fully converted tree stops reporting its own output as unknown (71 warnings on a clean port).
- [#523](../../issues/523) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A translation batch the model keeps collapsing is re-asked in halves instead of failing the page: a merged pair returns the same short array on every retry, which shipped `/blog/ship-the-docs-with-the-diff` untranslated in both languages. A single string that still will not come back whole fails loudly.
- [#504](../../issues/504) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The root layout stops stamping `data-theme-target`, a dead attribute interpolated from a legacy key the config schema never had - nothing reads it, and a correct brand file rendered it empty.
- [#485](../../issues/485) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A DSN at `monitoring.providers.sentry` now wins the page `Configuration` blob: the web foot emits it AFTER the `resolved.client` loop, so a stale `client.sentry` stops silently overwriting the DSN the monitoring service provisioned. A brand with no canonical DSN keeps reporting from its legacy client blob exactly as before.
- [#531](../../issues/531) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The neobrutalism and newsflash `.omega-price-card` skins moved out of the /pricing page sheet into the theme's marketing layer, so the homepage plan band wears the theme's cards instead of a plain themed `card`; /pricing renders identically from the shared layer.
- [#530](../../issues/530) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The #512 sweep finishes: `marketing/showcase`, `marketing/product-demo` and `marketing/pricing-cards` ship an empty `subheadline` default, so a band composed without one renders no sub line instead of the section's demo sentence; each demo line moved to that section's gallery variant.
- [#524](../../issues/524) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The config schema declares the keys real brand files already carry - `brand.type`, `brand.font`, `brand.contact.phone`, `cloud.oauthRedirectsConfigured`, the google/meta/tiktok platform account ids and the chatsy owner account - so a typo in one fails loudly instead of validating by not being looked at, and each key documents what reads it.
- [#528](../../issues/528) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The backend event-handler recipe documents the real `src/manager/events/` tree and the shape EventMiddleware actually calls (an async function taking `{ Manager, ctx, libraries, user, context, change, snapshot }`), so a handler written to the doc stops silently never running; the consumer lane and its src-first authored path are named too.
- [#532](../../issues/532) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every refund lookup is linked back to the resource the event named - PayPal's `sale_id`/`links[rel=up]`, Chargebee's `subscription_id`/`reference_invoice_id`, Stripe's charge `subscription`/`metadata.uid` - and a record belonging to another order is refused, stamped `refund-not-linked`, so an unrelated refund id stops importing another customer's amount onto this order.
- [#533](../../issues/533) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Chargebee payload uid is checked against the hosted page's `pass_thru_content` BEFORE it is allowed to steer: a page that names another owner refuses the event, and a page outside the 25-entry scan window still falls back to the payload with the loud warn.
- [#534](../../issues/534) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Chargebee refund with no credit note reads its amount and currency from a trusted `GET /transactions/{id}` (the payload's transaction id as a lookup key only), so a genuine gateway refund stops recording `amount: null` on the order and in the customer's refund email.
- [#535](../../issues/535) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A REFUSED webhook whose refusal stamp fails to write no longer falls back to marking a payload-named `payments-intents` doc failed: the refusal is terminal, nothing else is written, and the retry sweep re-lands the stamp.
- [#536](../../issues/536) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A Stripe refund envelope carrying no charge id fails as a PERMANENT failure before any `charges.retrieve` call - dead-lettered on the first attempt with the envelope's actual shape in the log - instead of classifying as unreachable and burning the whole retry ladder.
- [#541](../../issues/541) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The `page.<key>` codemod tracks Liquid block scope: inside a `{% for %}`/`{% tablerow %}` binding the same name the rename is skipped and reported with file, line and hand-port, so `{% if post.id != page.post.id %}` stops becoming a self-comparison. Occurrences outside the loop still rewrite.
- [#545](../../issues/545) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` maps the three renamed section args - `hero.tagline` → `hero.badge.text`, `cta.description` → `cta.subheadline`, `cta.button` → `cta.primary_button` - so a migrated page keeps its eyebrow, CTA sub line and CTA button. A hero already carrying the legacy `badge:` object is reported for a by-hand merge.
- [#551](../../issues/551) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Two default-true switches read backwards now read `!== false`: the web consent gate (`client.consent.enabled`) and the monthly marketing prune (`marketing.prune.enabled`) stay ON for a config that never mentions the key, so a config built outside the loader stops silently dropping the banner and skipping the prune.
- [#556](../../issues/556) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The generated `/ads.txt` opens on its record: the gate tag right-strips too, so every brand's file stops starting with a blank line. The no-provider comment gets the same treatment.
- [#553](../../issues/553) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `devlog.enabled` is declared in the config schema - boolean, optional, no materialized default - so a misspelled switch fails validation instead of validating clean. Absence still means off; only a literal `true` publishes devlog posts.
- [#542](../../issues/542) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Frontmatter and `.11tydata.*` sidecar Liquid renders with the consumer's own `_data` globals in scope - the same globals a template render gets, `site` included - so `{{ brands.size }}` beside a `_data/brands.json` stops rendering empty with no warning.
- [#543](../../issues/543) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The sidecar array-replace repair covers every extension: a `.11tydata.js`/`.cjs` sidecar array REPLACES the layout default exactly as the `.json` twin does, instead of concatenating a 6-item list onto the default into 7.
- [#544](../../issues/544) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A `meta:` ref rooted at the page's pagination alias defers to render time; any other meta ref with an undefined root fails the build naming file and ref, never caching an empty render. Empty optional values still pass.
- [#557](../../issues/557) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - A rejected reCAPTCHA verify prints Google's verdict as ONE warn line - `error-codes`, `score`, `action`, `hostname`, never the token or secret - so a captcha 403 names its own reason. The boolean return is unchanged; a pass stays silent.
- [#558](../../issues/558) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The checkout order summary prices a one-time product by its one-time price (`$49.99 one-time`) instead of the URL's default cadence, which read `$0.00 annually` over a $49.99 SUBTOTAL. Subscriptions keep their `$X annually|monthly` line.
- [#526](../../issues/526) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega migrate` converts `translation` to the shared ROOT, not `targets.web`, finishing the #483 shape: disperse copies the SHARED sections, so a target-scoped engine config never reached the other targets that translate. Doc row and converter now say one thing.
- [#566](../../issues/566) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The response-headers doc names `ctx.report()`, the method that exists: the header line stopped pointing a porting consumer at BEM's removed `errorify()`, which throws `TypeError` on the first error it handles.
- [#574](../../issues/574) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension docs and the scaffolded manifest comment describe what packaging does today: the firefox `gecko.id` is DERIVED from the brand when none is declared, failing only with no brand facts at all. The "declare it before the first publish" caveat stays, with its reason.
- [#572](../../issues/572) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` writes an extension consumer's `package.json` with npm's own trailing newline, and only when the content changed. Every build re-stripped that newline, so a consumer's editor or lint hook fought setup on every run.
- [#575](../../issues/575) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension boot layer skips as its docs promise when nothing is built: a directory qualifies only when its `manifest.json` is strict JSON, so the JSON5 `dist/` copy left by any dev run stops being loaded and failing every boot test. An explicitly named `OMEGA_TEST_BOOT_DIR` still fails loudly.
- [#582](../../issues/582) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The extension publish workflow injects `GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_SECRET`, so a CI-published extension stops baking an empty Measurement Protocol secret and sending no events.
- [#571](../../issues/571) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - An extension's build hooks run again: the package task resolves the nested `hooks/build/pre.js` the scaffold writes and setup migrates to, keeping the flat `hooks/build:pre.js` as a transition fallback. A miss logs through the framework logger, not an untagged `console.warn`, and the live-reload skip names the mode it skips in.
- [#568](../../issues/568) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The scaffolded web CI workflow stops filling the runner's disk (UJM 1.9.33's fix, ported): `fetch-depth: 1`, `force_orphan: true` on the gh-pages step, `df -h /` before and after the build (after with `if: always()`), and `git ls-files` as the file listing. **The next deploy discards gh-pages history.**
- [#562](../../issues/562) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The /pricing comparison matrix reads a cell the way the plan cards do: a truthy cell draws the check, and anything that is not a bare `true` prints its value beside it. One `features[].value` feeds both surfaces, so `"Included"` stops mixing with x icons in its own row.
- [#563](../../issues/563) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The alternatives hub prints each competitor's authored `description` under its row title, muted and unweighted, above the "View comparison" link. Every `_alternatives` doc writes the key and legacy UJM rendered it; the hub had nowhere for it to land. A competitor without one renders the row unchanged.
- [#561](../../issues/561) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - The download and extension shortlinks generate again: `/download/<platform>`, `/download/<platform>/<artifact>` and `/extension/<store>`, one redirect page per entry of the maps `/download` and `/extension` already render from. Legacy parity - a bare platform URL leads with its first artifact. Brands never hand-author these.
- [#580](../../issues/580) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - Every section arg naming a literal VALUE escapes on the way out - stat and hero card numbers, timeline years, the hero/cta command, bento's panels, the price card's catalog value - so `<200ms` stops opening a tag the minifier eats. Prose args keep their authored `<em>`.
- [#590](../../issues/590) [`11c2d35a`](../../commit/11c2d35a) Thanks [@ianwieds]! - `omega setup` writes a desktop consumer's `package.json` with npm's own trailing newline, and only when the content changed - the twin of #572. Every build re-stripped that newline, so a consumer's editor or lint hook fought setup on every run.
